                ImportedAttachmentsCount = importedAttachments
            };
        }

        private static string SanitizeFileName(string name)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name)) return "Item";
            var invalid = Path.GetInvalidFileNameChars();
            var cleaned = new string(name.Select(ch => invalid.Contains(ch) || ch == '/' || ch == '\\' ? '_' : ch).ToArray()).Trim();
            return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cleaned) ? "Item" : cleaned.Length > 60 ? cleaned.Substring(0, 60) : cleaned;
        }

        public async Task<DashboardStatistics> GetDashboardStatisticsAsync()
        {
            using var db = new ArchiveDbContext();
            var today = DateTime.Today;
            var tomorrow = today.AddDays(1);

            int total = await db.Documents.CountAsync();
            int inbound = await db.Documents.CountAsync(d => d.Type == DocumentType.Inbound);
            int outbound = await db.Documents.CountAsync(d => d.Type == DocumentType.Outbound);
            int todayCount = await db.Documents.CountAsync(d => d.RegistrationDate >= today && d.RegistrationDate < tomorrow);
            int urgent = await db.Documents.CountAsync(d => d.Priority == DocumentPriority.Urgent || d.Priority == DocumentPriority.VeryUrgent);
            int attachments = await db.Attachments.CountAsync();
            long totalStorage = _fileStorageService.GetTotalStorageSizeBytes();

            return new DashboardStatistics
            {
                TotalDocuments = total,
                InboundCount = inbound,
                OutboundCount = outbound,
                TodayCount = todayCount,
                UrgentCount = urgent,
                TotalAttachmentsCount = attachments,
                TotalStorageBytes = totalStorage
            };
        }

        public async Task<DocumentAttachment> AddAttachmentAsync(int documentId, string filePath, bool isScanned = false)
        {
            using var db = new ArchiveDbContext();
            var attachment = await _fileStorageService.SaveFileAttachmentAsync(filePath, documentId, isScanned);
            await db.Attachments.AddAsync(attachment);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            return attachment;
        }

        public async Task<bool> DeleteAttachmentAsync(int attachmentId)
        {
            using var db = new ArchiveDbContext();
            var att = await db.Attachments.FindAsync(attachmentId);
            if (att == null) return false;

            _fileStorageService.DeleteFile(att.StoragePath);
            db.Attachments.Remove(att);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            return true;
        }
    }
}
Services\FileStorageService.cs
AES-256-CBC storage service encrypting .denc files inside the Windows Vault folder to block external access, while automatically decrypting in RAM for internal viewing (active dialog Owner resolution).
محرك التخزين المشفر AES-256-CBC بمفتاح وترويسة DOCUARCH_ENC_V1! لحماية الملفات داخل مجلد الويندوز فقط ضد الوصول الخارجي وفك تشفيرها تلقائياً عند العرض داخل التطبيق.
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using DocuArchivePro.Data;
using DocuArchivePro.Models;
using DocuArchivePro.Views;

namespace DocuArchivePro.Services
{
    public class FileStorageService : IFileStorageService
    {
        // 16-byte magic header identifying encrypted archive files
        private static readonly byte[] MagicHeader = Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes("DOCUARCH_ENC_V1!");

        // 256-bit internal encryption key derived from application passphrase
        private static readonly byte[] AesKey = SHA256.HashData(
            Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes("HussamBajawi_DocuArchivePro_2026_MasterVaultSecretKey!@#"));

        private string? _cachedRootDirectory;

        public string StorageRootDirectory
        {
            get
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_cachedRootDirectory))
                {
                    try
                    {
                        using var db = new ArchiveDbContext();
                        var settings = db.SystemSettings.FirstOrDefault();
                        if (settings != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(settings.VaultStoragePath))
                        {
                            _cachedRootDirectory = settings.VaultStoragePath;
                        }
                    }
                    catch
                    {
                        // Fallback if DB not yet initialized
                    }
                }

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_cachedRootDirectory))
                {
                    _cachedRootDirectory = Path.Combine(
                        Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData),
                        "DocuArchivePro",
                        "Vault");
                }

                if (!Directory.Exists(_cachedRootDirectory))
                {
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(_cachedRootDirectory);
                }

                return _cachedRootDirectory;
            }
        }

        public async Task<DocumentAttachment> SaveFileAttachmentAsync(string sourceFilePath, int documentId, bool isScanned = false)
        {
            if (!File.Exists(sourceFilePath))
            {
                throw new FileNotFoundException("الملف المصدر غير موجود", sourceFilePath);
            }

            byte[] bytes = await File.ReadAllBytesAsync(sourceFilePath);
            string originalFileName = Path.GetFileName(sourceFilePath);
            return await SaveByteArrayAttachmentAsync(bytes, originalFileName, documentId, isScanned);
        }

        public async Task<DocumentAttachment> SaveByteArrayAttachmentAsync(byte[] fileBytes, string originalFileName, int documentId, bool isScanned = true)
        {
            string ext = Path.GetExtension(originalFileName).ToLowerInvariant();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(ext)) ext = ".png";

            // Compute SHA256 Hash of original unencrypted content
            byte[] hashBytes = SHA256.HashData(fileBytes);
            string hashStr = BitConverter.ToString(hashBytes).Replace("-", "").ToLowerInvariant();

            // Store in Year/Month structured subfolder inside the chosen Vault directory
            string yearMonthFolder = Path.Combine(StorageRootDirectory, DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyy"), DateTime.Now.ToString("MM"));
            if (!Directory.Exists(yearMonthFolder))
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(yearMonthFolder);
            }

            // Save with encrypted .denc extension so external apps cannot recognize or open it
            string uniqueFileName = $"{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd_HHmmss}_{Guid.NewGuid():N}.denc";
            string destinationPath = Path.Combine(yearMonthFolder, uniqueFileName);

            byte[] encryptedPayload = EncryptBytes(fileBytes);
            await File.WriteAllBytesAsync(destinationPath, encryptedPayload);

            return new DocumentAttachment
            {
                DocumentId = documentId,
                FileName = originalFileName,
                StoragePath = destinationPath,
                FileExtension = ext,
                FileSizeBytes = fileBytes.Length,
                FileHashSha256 = hashStr,
                IsScanned = isScanned,
                UploadedAt = DateTime.Now
            };
        }

        public static byte[] EncryptBytes(byte[] plainBytes)
        {
            using var aes = Aes.Create();
            aes.Key = AesKey;
            aes.GenerateIV();
            aes.Mode = CipherMode.CBC;
            aes.Padding = PaddingMode.PKCS7;

            using var ms = new MemoryStream();
            // 1. Write 16-byte Magic Header
            ms.Write(MagicHeader, 0, MagicHeader.Length);
            // 2. Write 16-byte Random IV
            ms.Write(aes.IV, 0, aes.IV.Length);

            // 3. Write AES-256 Encrypted Payload
            using (var encryptor = aes.CreateEncryptor())
            using (var cs = new CryptoStream(ms, encryptor, CryptoStreamMode.Write))
            {
                cs.Write(plainBytes, 0, plainBytes.Length);
                cs.FlushFinalBlock();
            }

            return ms.ToArray();
        }

        public static byte[] DecryptBytes(byte[] fileOnDisk)
        {
            if (!IsEncryptedPayload(fileOnDisk))
            {
                return fileOnDisk; // Legacy unencrypted file fallback
            }

            byte[] iv = new byte[16];
            Buffer.BlockCopy(fileOnDisk, MagicHeader.Length, iv, 0, 16);

            int cipherOffset = MagicHeader.Length + 16;
            int cipherLength = fileOnDisk.Length - cipherOffset;

            using var aes = Aes.Create();
            aes.Key = AesKey;
            aes.IV = iv;
            aes.Mode = CipherMode.CBC;
            aes.Padding = PaddingMode.PKCS7;

            using var inputMs = new MemoryStream(fileOnDisk, cipherOffset, cipherLength);
            using var decryptor = aes.CreateDecryptor();
            using var cs = new CryptoStream(inputMs, decryptor, CryptoStreamMode.Read);
            using var outputMs = new MemoryStream();
            cs.CopyTo(outputMs);
            return outputMs.ToArray();
        }

        public static bool IsEncryptedPayload(byte[] data)
        {
            if (data == null || data.Length <= MagicHeader.Length + 16) return false;
            for (int i = 0; i < MagicHeader.Length; i++)
            {
                if (data[i] != MagicHeader[i]) return false;
            }
            return true;
        }

        public async Task<byte[]> GetDecryptedBytesAsync(string storagePath)
        {
            if (!File.Exists(storagePath))
            {
                throw new FileNotFoundException("لم يتم العثور على الملف المؤرشف في المستودع", storagePath);
            }

            byte[] diskBytes = await File.ReadAllBytesAsync(storagePath);
            return DecryptBytes(diskBytes);
        }

        public void OpenAttachmentInApp(DocumentAttachment attachment)
        {
            try
            {
                if (!File.Exists(attachment.StoragePath))
                {
                    MessageBox.Show($"الملف المؤرشف غير موجود في المسار:\n{attachment.StoragePath}", "خطأ في فتح المرفق", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                    return;
                }

                byte[] diskBytes = File.ReadAllBytes(attachment.StoragePath);
                byte[] decrypted = DecryptBytes(diskBytes);

                var viewer = new AttachmentViewerDialog(decrypted, attachment.FileName, attachment.FileHashSha256)
                {
                    Owner = Application.Current.Windows.OfType<Window>().FirstOrDefault(w => w.IsActive) ?? Application.Current.MainWindow
                };
                viewer.ShowDialog();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"تعذر فك تشفير أو استعراض الملف:\n{ex.Message}", "خطأ أمني", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
            }
        }

        public void OpenFileInDefaultViewer(string storagePath)
        {
            try
            {
                using var db = new ArchiveDbContext();
                var att = db.Attachments.FirstOrDefault(a => a.StoragePath == storagePath);
                if (att != null)
                {
                    OpenAttachmentInApp(att);
                    return;
                }

                if (!File.Exists(storagePath))
                {
                    MessageBox.Show("لم يتم العثور على الملف في الأرشيف", "تنبيه", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                    return;
                }

                byte[] diskBytes = File.ReadAllBytes(storagePath);
                byte[] decrypted = DecryptBytes(diskBytes);
                string fileName = Path.GetFileName(storagePath);

                var viewer = new AttachmentViewerDialog(decrypted, fileName)
                {
                    Owner = Application.Current.Windows.OfType<Window>().FirstOrDefault(w => w.IsActive) ?? Application.Current.MainWindow
                };
                viewer.ShowDialog();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"تعذر فتح الملف المؤرشف:\n{ex.Message}", "خطأ", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
            }
        }

        public async Task<int> ChangeVaultDirectoryAsync(string newDirectoryPath, bool migrateExistingFiles = true)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(newDirectoryPath))
                throw new ArgumentException("يرجى تحديد مسار صالح للمجلد");

            if (!Directory.Exists(newDirectoryPath))
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(newDirectoryPath);
            }

            string oldRoot = StorageRootDirectory;
            int migratedCount = 0;

            using var db = new ArchiveDbContext();

            if (migrateExistingFiles && !string.Equals(oldRoot, newDirectoryPath, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                var attachments = await db.Attachments.ToListAsync();
                foreach (var att in attachments)
                {
                    if (File.Exists(att.StoragePath))
                    {
                        byte[] rawOrEnc = await File.ReadAllBytesAsync(att.StoragePath);
                        byte[] encrypted = IsEncryptedPayload(rawOrEnc) ? rawOrEnc : EncryptBytes(rawOrEnc);

                        string yearMonth = Path.Combine(newDirectoryPath, att.UploadedAt.ToString("yyyy"), att.UploadedAt.ToString("MM"));
                        if (!Directory.Exists(yearMonth)) Directory.CreateDirectory(yearMonth);

                        string newFileName = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(att.StoragePath) + ".denc";
                        string newFilePath = Path.Combine(yearMonth, newFileName);

                        await File.WriteAllBytesAsync(newFilePath, encrypted);
                        try { File.Delete(att.StoragePath); } catch { }

                        att.StoragePath = newFilePath;
                        migratedCount++;
                    }
                }
                await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            }

            var settings = await SecurityService.Instance.GetSettingsAsync();
            settings.VaultStoragePath = newDirectoryPath;
            await SecurityService.Instance.SaveSettingsAsync(settings);

            _cachedRootDirectory = newDirectoryPath;
            return migratedCount;
        }

        public async Task<int> EncryptExistingUnencryptedFilesAsync()
        {
            int encryptedCount = 0;
            using var db = new ArchiveDbContext();
            var attachments = await db.Attachments.ToListAsync();

            foreach (var att in attachments)
            {
                if (File.Exists(att.StoragePath))
                {
                    byte[] data = await File.ReadAllBytesAsync(att.StoragePath);
                    if (!IsEncryptedPayload(data))
                    {
                        byte[] encrypted = EncryptBytes(data);
                        string newPath = Path.ChangeExtension(att.StoragePath, ".denc");
                        await File.WriteAllBytesAsync(newPath, encrypted);
                        if (!string.Equals(att.StoragePath, newPath, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        {
                            try { File.Delete(att.StoragePath); } catch { }
                        }
                        att.StoragePath = newPath;
                        encryptedCount++;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (encryptedCount > 0)
            {
                await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            }

            return encryptedCount;
        }

        public bool DeleteFile(string storagePath)
        {
            try
            {
                if (File.Exists(storagePath))
                {
                    File.Delete(storagePath);
                    return true;
                }
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                // File locked or permission error
            }
            return false;
        }

        public long GetTotalStorageSizeBytes()
        {
            if (!Directory.Exists(StorageRootDirectory)) return 0;
            long total = 0;
            var dir = new DirectoryInfo(StorageRootDirectory);
            foreach (var file in dir.GetFiles("*.*", SearchOption.AllDirectories))
            {
                total += file.Length;
            }
            return total;
        }
    }
}
Services\LocalizationService.cs
Singleton localization provider loading JSON dictionaries, switching FlowDirection/IsArabic, and synchronizing global UiScale.
خدمة الترجمة الفورية وقاموسي العربية/الإنجليزية وإدارة التكبير UiScale وتنسيق النسبة المئوية.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Windows;
using DocuArchivePro.Models;

namespace DocuArchivePro.Services
{
    public class LocalizationService : ILocalizationService
    {
        private static LocalizationService? _instance;
        public static LocalizationService Instance => _instance ??= new LocalizationService();

        private readonly Dictionary<string, Dictionary<string, string>> _translations = new();
        private AppLanguage _currentLanguage;
        private double _uiScale = 1.0;

        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;

        public AppLanguage CurrentLanguage
        {
            get => _currentLanguage;
            set
            {
                if (value != null && _currentLanguage != value)
                {
                    _currentLanguage = value;
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CurrentLanguage));
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsArabic));
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CurrentFlowDirection));
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(UiScalePercentText));
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ThemeButtonLabel));
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CurrentThemeStatusText));
                    OnPropertyChanged("Item[]"); // Notifies WPF bindings of dictionary changes

                    // Dynamically apply corresponding font family and numerals (Arabic vs Default English)
                    try
                    {
                        FontService.Instance.ApplyLanguage(value.Code);
                        ThemeService.Instance.NotifyThemeBindings();
                    }
                    catch
                    {
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        public bool IsArabic =>
            _currentLanguage.Code.Equals("ar", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        public FlowDirection CurrentFlowDirection =>
            _currentLanguage.IsRightToLeft ? FlowDirection.RightToLeft : FlowDirection.LeftToRight;

        public double UiScale
        {
            get => _uiScale;
            set
            {
                double clamped = Math.Round(Math.Clamp(value, 0.75, 1.50), 2);
                if (Math.Abs(_uiScale - clamped) > 0.001)
                {
                    _uiScale = clamped;
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(UiScale));
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(UiScalePercentText));
                }
            }
        }

        public string UiScalePercentText =>
            DateTimeHelper.FormatDigits($"{(int)Math.Round(UiScale * 100)}%", IsArabic);

        public bool IsDarkMode => ThemeService.Instance.IsDarkMode;
        public string ThemeButtonLabel => ThemeService.Instance.ThemeButtonLabel;
        public string CurrentThemeStatusText => ThemeService.Instance.CurrentThemeStatusText;

        public void ToggleTheme()
        {
            ThemeService.Instance.ToggleTheme();
        }

        public void SetTheme(bool isDark)
        {
            ThemeService.Instance.SetTheme(isDark);
        }

        public IReadOnlyList<AppLanguage> SupportedLanguages { get; } = new List<AppLanguage>
        {
            new AppLanguage { Code = "ar", NativeName = "العربية", EnglishName = "Arabic", IsRightToLeft = true, FlagIcon = "🇸🇦" },
            new AppLanguage { Code = "en", NativeName = "English (الإنجليزية)", EnglishName = "English", IsRightToLeft = false, FlagIcon = "🇺🇸" }
        };

        public string this[string key] => GetString(key);

        public LocalizationService()
        {
            _currentLanguage = SupportedLanguages[0]; // Arabic default as requested
            LoadLanguageFiles();
            ThemeService.Instance.PropertyChanged += (s, e) =>
            {
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsDarkMode));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ThemeButtonLabel));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CurrentThemeStatusText));
            };
        }

        private void LoadLanguageFiles()
        {
            string appDir = AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory;
            string langFolder = Path.Combine(appDir, "Resources", "Languages");

            foreach (var lang in SupportedLanguages)
            {
                string filePath = Path.Combine(langFolder, $"Strings.{lang.Code}.json");
                if (File.Exists(filePath))
                {
                    try
                    {
                        string json = File.ReadAllText(filePath);
                        var dict = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<Dictionary<string, string>>(json);
                        if (dict != null)
                        {
                            _translations[lang.Code] = dict;
                        }
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex)
                    {
                        System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"Failed to load language {lang.Code}: {ex.Message}");
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        public void SetLanguage(string languageCode)
        {
            var target = SupportedLanguages.FirstOrDefault(l => l.Code.Equals(languageCode, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
            if (target != null)
            {
                CurrentLanguage = target;
            }
        }

        public string GetString(string key)
        {
            if (_translations.TryGetValue(_currentLanguage.Code, out var dict) && dict.TryGetValue(key, out var val))
            {
                return val;
            }

            // Fallback to Arabic or English
            if (_translations.TryGetValue("ar", out var arDict) && arDict.TryGetValue(key, out var arVal))
            {
                return arVal;
            }

            if (_translations.TryGetValue("en", out var enDict) && enDict.TryGetValue(key, out var enVal))
            {
                return enVal;
            }

            return key; // return key itself if not translated
        }

        protected void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
        {
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
        }
    }
}
Services\FontService.cs
Discovers ArabicFonts fonts, applies FontFamily, NumberSubstitution, FlowDirection, ScaleTransform, and #1/#2 Window.Icon across every open window.
محرك الخطوط العربية الحصرية من مجلد ArabicFonts، وضبط الأرقام والاتجاه وأيقونات النوافذ (#1 و#2) وتكبير Ctrl+Wheel.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Data;
using System.Windows.Input;
using System.Windows.Markup;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using DocuArchivePro.Data;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace DocuArchivePro.Services
{
    public class ArabicFontItem : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        public string Key { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string ArabicDisplayName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string EnglishDisplayName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string FontFamilyName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string FolderPath { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public FontFamily FontFamily { get; set; } = null!;

        public string DisplayName => LocalizationService.Instance.IsArabic ? ArabicDisplayName : EnglishDisplayName;

        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;
        public void RefreshDisplayName() => PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(nameof(DisplayName)));

        public override string ToString() => DisplayName;
    }

    public class FontService
    {
        private static FontService? _instance;
        public static FontService Instance => _instance ??= new FontService();

        private readonly List<ArabicFontItem> _fonts = new();
        public IReadOnlyList<ArabicFontItem> AvailableArabicFonts => _fonts;

        private ArabicFontItem? _currentArabicFont;
        public ArabicFontItem? CurrentArabicFont
        {
            get => _currentArabicFont;
            set
            {
                if (value != null && _currentArabicFont != value)
                {
                    _currentArabicFont = value;
                }
            }
        }

        public static readonly FontFamily DefaultEnglishFont = new FontFamily("Segoe UI, Tahoma, Arial");

        public FontService()
        {
            DiscoverFonts();
        }

        public void DiscoverFonts()
        {
            _fonts.Clear();

            string? fontsRoot = ResolveFontsDirectory();

            // 1. Alexandria
            string alexDir = fontsRoot != null && Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(fontsRoot, "Alexandria"))
                ? Path.Combine(fontsRoot, "Alexandria")
                : (fontsRoot != null && Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(fontsRoot, "Alexandria,Amiri,Amiri_Quran", "Alexandria", "static"))
                    ? Path.Combine(fontsRoot, "Alexandria,Amiri,Amiri_Quran", "Alexandria", "static")
                    : string.Empty);

            FontFamily alexFam = CreateFontFamily(alexDir, "Alexandria");
            _fonts.Add(new ArabicFontItem
            {
                Key = "Alexandria",
                ArabicDisplayName = "خط الإسكندرية الحديث (Alexandria)",
                EnglishDisplayName = "Alexandria Modern Arabic Font",
                FontFamilyName = "Alexandria",
                FolderPath = alexDir,
                FontFamily = alexFam
            });

            // 2. Amiri
            string amiriDir = fontsRoot != null && Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(fontsRoot, "Amiri"))
                ? Path.Combine(fontsRoot, "Amiri")
                : (fontsRoot != null && Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(fontsRoot, "Alexandria,Amiri,Amiri_Quran", "Amiri"))
                    ? Path.Combine(fontsRoot, "Alexandria,Amiri,Amiri_Quran", "Amiri")
                    : string.Empty);

            FontFamily amiriFam = CreateFontFamily(amiriDir, "Amiri");
            _fonts.Add(new ArabicFontItem
            {
                Key = "Amiri",
                ArabicDisplayName = "خط الأميري الكلاسيكي (Amiri)",
                EnglishDisplayName = "Amiri Classic Arabic Font",
                FontFamilyName = "Amiri",
                FolderPath = amiriDir,
                FontFamily = amiriFam
            });

            // 3. Amiri Quran
            string quranDir = fontsRoot != null && Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(fontsRoot, "Amiri_Quran"))
                ? Path.Combine(fontsRoot, "Amiri_Quran")
                : (fontsRoot != null && Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(fontsRoot, "Alexandria,Amiri,Amiri_Quran", "Amiri_Quran"))
                    ? Path.Combine(fontsRoot, "Alexandria,Amiri,Amiri_Quran", "Amiri_Quran")
                    : string.Empty);

            FontFamily quranFam = CreateFontFamily(quranDir, "Amiri Quran");
            _fonts.Add(new ArabicFontItem
            {
                Key = "Amiri_Quran",
                ArabicDisplayName = "خط أميري قرآن العثماني (Amiri Quran)",
                EnglishDisplayName = "Amiri Quran Calligraphic Font",
                FontFamilyName = "Amiri Quran",
                FolderPath = quranDir,
                FontFamily = quranFam
            });

            _currentArabicFont = _fonts[0]; // Default to Alexandria
        }

        private static string? ResolveFontsDirectory()
        {
            string candidate1 = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, "ArabicFonts");
            if (Directory.Exists(candidate1)) return candidate1;

            string candidate2 = @"C:\Users\Pc\Desktop\DocuArchivePro\ArabicFonts";
            if (Directory.Exists(candidate2)) return candidate2;

            string? cur = AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory;
            while (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cur))
            {
                string c = Path.Combine(cur, "ArabicFonts");
                if (Directory.Exists(c)) return c;
                var parent = Directory.GetParent(cur);
                cur = parent?.FullName;
            }

            return null;
        }

        private static FontFamily CreateFontFamily(string dirPath, string familyName)
        {
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dirPath) && Directory.Exists(dirPath))
            {
                try
                {
                    string uriString = "file:///" + dirPath.Replace('\\', '/').TrimEnd('/') + "/";
                    var baseUri = new Uri(uriString);
                    return new FontFamily(baseUri, $"./#{familyName}, Segoe UI, Tahoma, Cairo, Arial");
                }
                catch
                {
                }
            }
            return new FontFamily($"{familyName}, Segoe UI, Tahoma, Cairo, Arial");
        }

        public async Task LoadSettingsAsync()
        {
            try
            {
                using var db = new ArchiveDbContext();
                var settings = await db.SystemSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();
                if (settings != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(settings.SelectedArabicFont))
                {
                    var match = _fonts.FirstOrDefault(f => f.Key.Equals(settings.SelectedArabicFont, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                                                           f.FontFamilyName.Equals(settings.SelectedArabicFont, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
                    if (match != null)
                    {
                        _currentArabicFont = match;
                    }
                }
            }
            catch
            {
            }
        }

        public async Task SaveSelectedFontAsync(string fontKey)
        {
            var match = _fonts.FirstOrDefault(f => f.Key.Equals(fontKey, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                                                   f.FontFamilyName.Equals(fontKey, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
            if (match != null)
            {
                _currentArabicFont = match;
                try
                {
                    using var db = new ArchiveDbContext();
                    var settings = await db.SystemSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();
                    if (settings != null)
                    {
                        settings.SelectedArabicFont = match.Key;
                        settings.UpdatedAt = DateTime.Now;
                        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
                    }
                }
                catch
                {
                }

                var loc = LocalizationService.Instance;
                if (loc.IsArabic)
                {
                    ApplyLanguage("ar");
                }
            }
        }

        public void ApplyLanguage(string languageCode)
        {
            bool isArabic = languageCode.Equals("ar", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            // 1. Update dynamic AppFontFamily in Application Resources
            FontFamily targetFamily = isArabic
                ? (_currentArabicFont?.FontFamily ?? _fonts[0].FontFamily)
                : DefaultEnglishFont;

            if (Application.Current != null)
            {
                Application.Current.Resources["AppFontFamily"] = targetFamily;
            }

            // Refresh localized font item names
            foreach (var f in _fonts)
            {
                f.RefreshDisplayName();
            }

            // 2. Apply NumberSubstitution, Language & FlowDirection to all open windows
            if (Application.Current != null && Application.Current.Windows != null)
            {
                foreach (Window window in Application.Current.Windows)
                {
                    ApplyToWindow(window, isArabic);
                }
            }
        }

        public void ApplyToWindow(Window window, bool? isArabicParam = null)
        {
            if (window == null) return;

            bool isArabic = isArabicParam ?? LocalizationService.Instance.IsArabic;

            if (isArabic)
            {
                NumberSubstitution.SetCultureSource(window, NumberCultureSource.Override);
                NumberSubstitution.SetCultureOverride(window, new CultureInfo("ar-SA"));
                NumberSubstitution.SetSubstitution(window, NumberSubstitutionMethod.Traditional);
                window.Language = XmlLanguage.GetLanguage("ar-SA");
                window.FlowDirection = FlowDirection.RightToLeft;
            }
            else
            {
                NumberSubstitution.SetCultureSource(window, NumberCultureSource.Override);
                NumberSubstitution.SetCultureOverride(window, new CultureInfo("en-US"));
                NumberSubstitution.SetSubstitution(window, NumberSubstitutionMethod.European);
                window.Language = XmlLanguage.GetLanguage("en-US");
                window.FlowDirection = FlowDirection.LeftToRight;
            }

            window.UseLayoutRounding = true;
            window.SnapsToDevicePixels = true;
            TextOptions.SetTextFormattingMode(window, TextFormattingMode.Ideal);
            TextOptions.SetTextRenderingMode(window, TextRenderingMode.ClearType);

            try
            {
                bool isSecurityWindow =
                    window is Views.ActionPinDialog ||
                    window is Views.LoginPromptDialog ||
                    window is Views.UserLoginDialog ||
                    window is Views.DeleteCategoryWizardDialog ||
                    window is Views.VaultSettingsDialog ||
                    window is Views.AddCategoryDialog ||
                    (window is Views.AddEntityDialog && !(SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser.IsAdmin || SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser.CanManageEntities));

                string iconUri = isSecurityWindow
                    ? "pack://application:,,,/Resources/Icons/SecurityLockIcon.png"
                    : "pack://application:,,,/Resources/Icons/AppIcon.png";
                window.Icon = BitmapFrame.Create(new Uri(iconUri, UriKind.Absolute));
            }
            catch
            {
            }

            // Bind root grid LayoutTransform to global UiScale so user-selected button/window size applies across all screens
            if (window.Content is FrameworkElement rootElement && rootElement.LayoutTransform is Transform existing && existing is not ScaleTransform)
            {
                // Leave custom non-scale transforms untouched
            }
            else if (window.Content is FrameworkElement rootEl && rootEl.LayoutTransform is Transform t && t == Transform.Identity)
            {
                var scale = new ScaleTransform();
                var loc = LocalizationService.Instance;
                BindingOperations.SetBinding(scale, ScaleTransform.ScaleXProperty, new Binding(nameof(LocalizationService.UiScale)) { Source = loc, Mode = BindingMode.OneWay });
                BindingOperations.SetBinding(scale, ScaleTransform.ScaleYProperty, new Binding(nameof(LocalizationService.UiScale)) { Source = loc, Mode = BindingMode.OneWay });
                rootEl.LayoutTransform = scale;
            }

            if (!HasWheelZoomHandler(window))
            {
                window.SetValue(HasWheelZoomAttachedProperty, true);
                window.PreviewMouseWheel += (s, e) =>
                {
                    if (Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.Control)
                    {
                        LocalizationService.Instance.UiScale += e.Delta > 0 ? 0.05 : -0.05;
                        e.Handled = true;
                    }
                };
            }

            ThemeService.Instance.ApplyThemeToWindow(window);
        }

        private static readonly DependencyProperty HasWheelZoomAttachedProperty =
            DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("HasWheelZoomAttached", typeof(bool), typeof(FontService), new PropertyMetadata(false));

        private static bool HasWheelZoomHandler(Window w) => (bool)w.GetValue(HasWheelZoomAttachedProperty);
    }
}
Services\DateTimeHelper.cs
Digit substitution helpers (Arabic-Indic vs Western digits) and bilingual Hijri/Gregorian date formatters.
تحويل الأرقام بين الهندية العربية (٠-٩) والغربية (0-9) وتنسيق التواريخ الهجرية (أم القرى) والميلادية.
using System;
using System.Globalization;

namespace DocuArchivePro.Services
{
    public static class DateTimeHelper
    {
        private static readonly string[] HijriMonthsAr =
        {
            "محرم", "صفر", "ربيع الأول", "ربيع الآخر",
            "جمادى الأولى", "جمادى الآخرة", "رجب", "شعبان",
            "رمضان", "شوال", "ذو القعدة", "ذو الحجة"
        };

        private static readonly string[] HijriMonthsEn =
        {
            "Muharram", "Safar", "Rabi' al-Awwal", "Rabi' al-Thani",
            "Jumada al-Awwal", "Jumada al-Thani", "Rajab", "Sha'ban",
            "Ramadan", "Shawwal", "Dhu al-Qi'dah", "Dhu al-Hijjah"
        };

        private static readonly string[] GregorianMonthsAr =
        {
            "يناير", "فبراير", "مارس", "أبريل", "مايو", "يونيو",
            "يوليو", "أغسطس", "سبتمبر", "أكتوبر", "نوفمبر", "ديسمبر"
        };

        private static readonly string[] ArabicDays =
        {
            "الأحد", "الإثنين", "الثلاثاء", "الأربعاء", "الخميس", "الجمعة", "السبت"
        };

        public static string ToArabicDigits(this string input)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input)) return input;
            return input
                .Replace('0', '٠')
                .Replace('1', '١')
                .Replace('2', '٢')
                .Replace('3', '٣')
                .Replace('4', '٤')
                .Replace('5', '٥')
                .Replace('6', '٦')
                .Replace('7', '٧')
                .Replace('8', '٨')
                .Replace('9', '٩');
        }

        public static string ToEuropeanDigits(this string input)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input)) return input;
            return input
                .Replace('٠', '0')
                .Replace('١', '1')
                .Replace('٢', '2')
                .Replace('٣', '3')
                .Replace('٤', '4')
                .Replace('٥', '5')
                .Replace('٦', '6')
                .Replace('٧', '7')
                .Replace('٨', '8')
                .Replace('٩', '9');
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Converts digits in a string to Arabic-Indic (٠-٩) when Arabic is active,
        /// or European digits (0-9) when English is active.
        /// </summary>
        public static string FormatDigits(string? input, bool? isArabic = null)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input)) return string.Empty;
            bool ar = isArabic ?? LocalizationService.Instance.IsArabic;
            return ar ? input.ToArabicDigits() : input.ToEuropeanDigits();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Formats any numeric or textual value with the active language's digit system.
        /// </summary>
        public static string FormatNumber(object? value, bool? isArabic = null)
        {
            if (value == null) return string.Empty;
            string str = value switch
            {
                IFormattable f => f.ToString(null, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
                _ => value.ToString() ?? string.Empty
            };
            return FormatDigits(str, isArabic);
        }

        public static string GetDayNameArabic(DateTime dt)
        {
            return ArabicDays[(int)dt.DayOfWeek];
        }

        public static string GetTimeArabic(DateTime dt)
        {
            int hour12 = dt.Hour % 12;
            if (hour12 == 0) hour12 = 12;
            string amPm = dt.Hour >= 12 ? "مساءً" : "صباحاً";
            string raw = $"{hour12:D2}:{dt.Minute:D2}:{dt.Second:D2} {amPm}";
            return raw.ToArabicDigits();
        }

        public static string GetGregorianDateArabic(DateTime dt)
        {
            string monthName = GregorianMonthsAr[dt.Month - 1];
            string raw = $"{dt.Day:D2} {monthName} {dt.Year} م ({dt:yyyy/MM/dd})";
            return raw.ToArabicDigits();
        }

        public static string GetHijriDateArabic(DateTime dt)
        {
            int hYear, hMonth, hDay;
            try
            {
                Calendar cal = new UmAlQuraCalendar();
                hYear = cal.GetYear(dt);
                hMonth = cal.GetMonth(dt);
                hDay = cal.GetDayOfMonth(dt);
            }
            catch
            {
                Calendar cal = new HijriCalendar();
                hYear = cal.GetYear(dt);
                hMonth = cal.GetMonth(dt);
                hDay = cal.GetDayOfMonth(dt);
            }
            string monthName = HijriMonthsAr[Math.Clamp(hMonth - 1, 0, 11)];
            string raw = $"{hDay:D2} {monthName} {hYear} هـ ({hYear:D4}/{hMonth:D2}/{hDay:D2} هـ)";
            return raw.ToArabicDigits();
        }

        // English format methods (using default European digits and Latin words)
        public static string GetDayNameEnglish(DateTime dt)
        {
            return dt.ToString("dddd", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        }

        public static string GetTimeEnglish(DateTime dt)
        {
            return dt.ToString("hh:mm:ss tt", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        }

        public static string GetGregorianDateEnglish(DateTime dt)
        {
            return dt.ToString("dd MMMM yyyy (yyyy/MM/dd)", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        }

        public static string GetHijriDateEnglish(DateTime dt)
        {
            int hYear, hMonth, hDay;
            try
            {
                Calendar cal = new UmAlQuraCalendar();
                hYear = cal.GetYear(dt);
                hMonth = cal.GetMonth(dt);
                hDay = cal.GetDayOfMonth(dt);
            }
            catch
            {
                Calendar cal = new HijriCalendar();
                hYear = cal.GetYear(dt);
                hMonth = cal.GetMonth(dt);
                hDay = cal.GetDayOfMonth(dt);
            }
            string monthName = HijriMonthsEn[Math.Clamp(hMonth - 1, 0, 11)];
            return $"{hDay:D2} {monthName} {hYear} AH ({hYear:D4}/{hMonth:D2}/{hDay:D2})";
        }
    }
}
Views\AddCategoryDialog.xaml.cs
Code-behind for Add/Edit Dossier dialog enforcing pre-configured Action PIN confirmation whenever modifying an existing dossier.
منطق إضافة وتعديل الإضبارة مع التحقق الإلزامي من كلمة السر عبر ActionPinDialog عند التعديل.
using System;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Windows;
using DocuArchivePro.Models;
using DocuArchivePro.Services;

namespace DocuArchivePro.Views
{
    public partial class AddCategoryDialog : Window, INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        private readonly IDocumentService _documentService;
        private readonly Category? _existingCategory;
        private readonly PropertyChangedEventHandler _locHandler;

        public ILocalizationService Loc => LocalizationService.Instance;
        public Category? CreatedCategory { get; private set; }
        public bool IsEditMode => _existingCategory != null;

        public string DialogTitle => Loc[IsEditMode ? "EditCatWindowTitle" : "AddCatWindowTitle"];
        public string HeaderText => Loc[IsEditMode ? "EditCatHeader" : "AddCatHeader"];
        public string SubheaderText => Loc[IsEditMode ? "EditCatSubheader" : "AddCatSubheader"];
        public string SaveButtonText => Loc[IsEditMode ? "EditCatSaveBtn" : "AddCatSaveBtn"];
        public string HeaderIconPath => "pack://application:,,,/Resources/Icons/SecurityLockIcon.png";
        public Visibility EditLockVisibility => Visibility.Visible;

        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;

        public AddCategoryDialog(IDocumentService documentService, Category? existingCategory = null)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _documentService = documentService;
            _existingCategory = existingCategory;
            DataContext = this;

            if (_existingCategory != null)
            {
                TxtCategoryName.Text = _existingCategory.Name;
                TxtCategoryDescription.Text = _existingCategory.Description;
            }

            _locHandler = (s, e) =>
            {
                if (e.PropertyName == nameof(ILocalizationService.CurrentLanguage))
                {
                    PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(nameof(DialogTitle)));
                    PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(nameof(HeaderText)));
                    PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(nameof(SubheaderText)));
                    PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(nameof(SaveButtonText)));
                }
            };
            Loc.PropertyChanged += _locHandler;
            Closed += (s, e) => Loc.PropertyChanged -= _locHandler;

            Loaded += (s, e) =>
            {
                TxtCategoryName.Focus();
                TxtCategoryName.SelectAll();
            };
        }

        private async void BtnSave_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            string name = TxtCategoryName.Text.Trim();
            string desc = TxtCategoryDescription.Text.Trim();

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name))
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    Loc.IsArabic ? "يرجى إدخال اسم الإضبارة أو التصنيف." : "Please enter a dossier or category name.",
                    Loc["Warning"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                TxtCategoryName.Focus();
                return;
            }

            try
            {
                if (_existingCategory != null)
                {
                    string actionTitle = Loc.IsArabic ? "تعديل الإضبارة / التصنيف" : "Edit Dossier / Category";
                    string warningMsg = Loc.IsArabic
                        ? $"هل أنت متأكد من رغبتك في تعديل بيانات هذه الإضبارة؟\n\nالاسم السابق: {_existingCategory.DisplayName}\nالاسم الجديد: {name}\n\nيتطلب تنفيذ التعديل إدخال كلمة السر المعدة مسبقاً."
                        : $"Are you sure you want to modify this dossier?\n\nPrevious Name: {_existingCategory.DisplayName}\nNew Name: {name}\n\nSaving changes requires the pre-configured security password.";

                    if (!ActionPinDialog.ConfirmWarningAndVerifyPin(actionTitle, warningMsg, this))
                    {
                        return;
                    }

                    _existingCategory.Name = name;
                    _existingCategory.Description = desc;
                    CreatedCategory = await _documentService.UpdateCategoryAsync(_existingCategory);
                }
                else
                {
                    string actionTitle = Loc.IsArabic ? "إضافة إضبارة / تصنيف جديد" : "Add New Dossier / Category";
                    string warningMsg = Loc.IsArabic
                        ? $"هل أنت متأكد من رغبتك في إضافة إضبارة جديدة للأرشيف؟\n\nاسم الإضبارة: {name}\n\nيتطلب إضافة إضبارة جديدة إدخال كلمة السر الخاصة بالحذف والتعديل المعدة مسبقاً."
                        : $"Are you sure you want to add this new archive dossier?\n\nDossier Name: {name}\n\nAdding a new dossier requires the pre-configured Edit/Delete security password.";

                    if (!ActionPinDialog.ConfirmWarningAndVerifyPin(actionTitle, warningMsg, this))
                    {
                        return;
                    }

                    var newCat = new Category
                    {
                        Name = name,
                        Description = desc,
                        IsActive = true
                    };

                    CreatedCategory = await _documentService.AddCategoryAsync(newCat);
                }

                DialogResult = true;
                Close();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    Loc.IsArabic ? $"فشل حفظ الإضبارة:\n{ex.Message}" : $"Failed to save dossier:\n{ex.Message}",
                    Loc["Error"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
            }
        }

        private void BtnCancel_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            DialogResult = false;
            Close();
        }
    }
}
Views\DeleteCategoryWizardDialog.xaml.cs
3-step Dossier Deletion Wizard controller: live DB impact warning, optional unencrypted backup export, and permanent cascading delete.
معالج الحذف الآمن للإضبارة المكون من 3 خطوات: التحذير والأعداد الحية، النسخ الاحتياطي غير المشفر، والحذف النهائي.
using System;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.IO;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Media;
using Microsoft.Win32;
using DocuArchivePro.Models;
using DocuArchivePro.Services;

namespace DocuArchivePro.Views
{
    public partial class DeleteCategoryWizardDialog : Window
    {
        private readonly IDocumentService _documentService;
        private readonly Category _category;
        private readonly PropertyChangedEventHandler _locHandler;

        private CategoryDeletionSummary? _summary;
        private CategoryBackupExportResult? _backupResult;
        private int _currentStep = 1;

        public ILocalizationService Loc => LocalizationService.Instance;

        public DeleteCategoryWizardDialog(IDocumentService documentService, Category category)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _documentService = documentService;
            _category = category;
            DataContext = this;

            string defaultRoot = Path.Combine(
                Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.DesktopDirectory),
                "DocuArchive_Backups");
            TxtBackupFolder.Text = defaultRoot;

            _locHandler = (s, e) =>
            {
                if (e.PropertyName == nameof(ILocalizationService.CurrentLanguage))
                {
                    RefreshDynamicTexts();
                }
            };
            Loc.PropertyChanged += _locHandler;
            Closed += (s, e) => Loc.PropertyChanged -= _locHandler;

            Loaded += async (s, e) => await LoadSummaryAsync();
        }

        private async Task LoadSummaryAsync()
        {
            try
            {
                _summary = await _documentService.GetCategoryDeletionSummaryAsync(_category.Id);
                RefreshDynamicTexts();
                SetStep(1);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    Loc.IsArabic ? $"تعذر قراءة بيانات الإضبارة:\n{ex.Message}" : $"Failed to load dossier summary:\n{ex.Message}",
                    Loc["Error"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
                DialogResult = false;
                Close();
            }
        }

        private void RefreshDynamicTexts()
        {
            if (_summary == null) return;

            bool isAr = Loc.IsArabic;
            string catName = _category.DisplayName;
            string inStr = DateTimeHelper.FormatNumber(_summary.InboundCount, isAr);
            string outStr = DateTimeHelper.FormatNumber(_summary.OutboundCount, isAr);
            string attStr = DateTimeHelper.FormatNumber(_summary.TotalAttachmentsCount, isAr);

            TxtInboundCountVal.Text = inStr;
            TxtOutboundCountVal.Text = outStr;
            TxtAttachmentsCountVal.Text = attStr;

            TxtStep1ImpactMessage.Text = isAr
                ? $"أنت الآن على وشك حذف الإضبارة: ({catName})\nوالمرتبط بها: معاملات وارد عدد ({inStr})، ومعاملات صادر عدد ({outStr})، وملفات مرفقة مؤرشفة عدد ({attStr})."
                : $"You are about to delete the dossier: ({catName})\nLinked records: Inbound ({inStr}), Outbound ({outStr}), Archived Attachments ({attStr}).";

            TxtStep3FinalImpact.Text = isAr
                ? $"سيتم حذف الإضبارة ({catName}) مع ({inStr}) معاملة وارد و({outStr}) معاملة صادر و({attStr}) مرفق مؤرشف نهائياً."
                : $"Dossier ({catName}) will be permanently deleted with ({inStr}) Inbound, ({outStr}) Outbound documents, and ({attStr}) archived files.";

            if (_backupResult != null)
            {
                BackupStatusBanner.Background = new SolidColorBrush((Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#DCFCE7"));
                BackupStatusBanner.BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush((Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#86EFAC"));
                TxtBackupStatusBanner.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush((Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#166534"));

                string docsExported = DateTimeHelper.FormatNumber(_backupResult.ExportedDocumentsCount, isAr);
                string filesExported = DateTimeHelper.FormatNumber(_backupResult.ExportedAttachmentsCount, isAr);
                TxtBackupStatusBanner.Text = isAr
                    ? $"✅ تم إنشاء نسخة احتياطية غير مشفرة بنجاح ({docsExported} معاملة، {filesExported} ملف) في المسار:\n{_backupResult.BackupFolderPath}"
                    : $"✅ Unencrypted backup created successfully ({docsExported} documents, {filesExported} files) at:\n{_backupResult.BackupFolderPath}";
            }
            else
            {
                BackupStatusBanner.Background = new SolidColorBrush((Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#FEF3C7"));
                BackupStatusBanner.BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush((Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#FCD34D"));
                TxtBackupStatusBanner.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush((Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#92400E"));

                TxtBackupStatusBanner.Text = isAr
                    ? "⚠️ تنبيه: لم يتم إنشاء نسخة احتياطية لهذه الإضبارة. في حال المتابعة لن تتمكن من استرجاع هذه الملفات لاحقاً."
                    : "⚠️ Notice: No backup was created for this dossier. If you proceed, these files cannot be recovered later.";
            }
        }

        private void SetStep(int step)
        {
            _currentStep = step;
            PanelStep1.Visibility = step == 1 ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
            PanelStep2.Visibility = step == 2 ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
            PanelStep3.Visibility = step == 3 ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;

            UpdateBadge(StepBadge1, TxtBadge1, step == 1, step > 1);
            UpdateBadge(StepBadge2, TxtBadge2, step == 2, step > 2);
            UpdateBadge(StepBadge3, TxtBadge3, step == 3, false);

            RefreshDynamicTexts();
            ThemeService.Instance.ApplyThemeToWindow(this);
        }

        private static void UpdateBadge(System.Windows.Controls.Border badge, System.Windows.Controls.TextBlock text, bool isActive, bool isDone)
        {
            if (isDone)
            {
                badge.Background = new SolidColorBrush((Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#DCFCE7"));
                badge.BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush((Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#22C55E"));
                text.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush((Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#15803D"));
                text.FontWeight = FontWeights.Bold;
            }
            else if (isActive)
            {
                badge.Background = new SolidColorBrush((Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#EFF6FF"));
                badge.BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush((Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#2563EB"));
                text.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush((Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#1E40AF"));
                text.FontWeight = FontWeights.Bold;
            }
            else
            {
                badge.Background = new SolidColorBrush((Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#F8FAFC"));
                badge.BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush((Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#CBD5E1"));
                text.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush((Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString("#64748B"));
                text.FontWeight = FontWeights.SemiBold;
            }
        }

        private void BtnStep1Next_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            SetStep(2);
        }

        private void BtnBrowseBackupFolder_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            var dlg = new OpenFolderDialog
            {
                Title = Loc.IsArabic ? "اختر مجلد حفظ النسخة الاحتياطية غير المشفرة للإضبارة" : "Select Folder for Unencrypted Dossier Backup",
                InitialDirectory = Directory.Exists(TxtBackupFolder.Text)
                    ? TxtBackupFolder.Text
                    : Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.DesktopDirectory)
            };

            if (dlg.ShowDialog(this) == true)
            {
                TxtBackupFolder.Text = dlg.FolderName;
            }
        }

        private async void BtnYesCreateBackup_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            string targetRoot = TxtBackupFolder.Text.Trim();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(targetRoot))
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    Loc.IsArabic ? "يرجى اختيار مجلد صالح لحفظ النسخة الاحتياطية." : "Please select a valid target folder for the backup.",
                    Loc["Warning"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                return;
            }

            try
            {
                BtnCreateBackup.IsEnabled = false;
                _backupResult = await _documentService.ExportCategoryBackupAsync(_category.Id, targetRoot);
                SetStep(3);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    Loc.IsArabic ? $"فشل إنشاء النسخة الاحتياطية:\n{ex.Message}" : $"Failed to create backup:\n{ex.Message}",
                    Loc["Error"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
            }
            finally
            {
                BtnCreateBackup.IsEnabled = true;
            }
        }

        private void BtnSkipBackup_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            _backupResult = null;
            SetStep(3);
        }

        private void BtnBackToStep1_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            SetStep(1);
        }

        private void BtnBackToStep2_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            SetStep(2);
        }

        private async void BtnDeletePermanently_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                BtnFinalDelete.IsEnabled = false;
                await _documentService.DeleteCategoryPermanentlyWithDocumentsAsync(_category.Id);

                bool isAr = Loc.IsArabic;
                string msg = isAr
                    ? $"تم حذف الإضبارة ({_category.DisplayName}) وجميع معاملاتها ومرفقاتها المؤرشفة نهائياً بنجاح."
                    : $"Dossier ({_category.DisplayName}) and all linked transactions and archived files were permanently deleted.";

                if (_backupResult != null)
                {
                    msg += isAr
                        ? $"\n\nتم الاحتفاظ بنسختك الاحتياطية غير المشفرة في:\n{_backupResult.BackupFolderPath}\nويمكنك استيرادها مستقبلاً من الإعدادات -> استيراد إضبارة احتياطية."
                        : $"\n\nYour unencrypted backup is saved at:\n{_backupResult.BackupFolderPath}\nYou can re-import it anytime from Settings -> Import Dossier Backup.";
                }

                MessageBox.Show(msg, Loc["Success"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
                DialogResult = true;
                Close();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    Loc.IsArabic ? $"حدث خطأ أثناء الحذف النهائي:\n{ex.Message}" : $"Error during permanent deletion:\n{ex.Message}",
                    Loc["Error"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
            }
            finally
            {
                BtnFinalDelete.IsEnabled = true;
            }
        }

        private void BtnCancel_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            DialogResult = false;
            Close();
        }
    }
}
Services\ThemeService.cs
Day/Night theme engine: swaps color dictionaries, persists the choice, and re-colors windows, popups, calendars and drop-downs while preserving DynamicResource/TemplateBinding values.
محرك الثيم الليلي/النهاري: تبديل قواميس الألوان، حفظ الاختيار في قاعدة البيانات، وتطبيق الألوان على النوافذ والقوائم المنبثقة والتقاويم دون المساس بالارتباطات DynamicResource/TemplateBinding.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Controls.Primitives;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using DocuArchivePro.Data;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace DocuArchivePro.Services
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Manages global Light (Day) and Dark (Night) themes across all windows, controls,
    /// DatePickers, Calendar popups, ComboBox dropdowns, ContextMenus, and ToolTips.
    /// Preserves all {DynamicResource} and {TemplateBinding} expressions across round-trip toggles.
    /// </summary>
    public class ThemeService : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        private static ThemeService? _instance;
        public static ThemeService Instance => _instance ??= new ThemeService();

        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;

        private bool _isDarkMode;
        public bool IsDarkMode => _isDarkMode;

        public string ThemeIcon => _isDarkMode ? "☀️" : "🌙";

        public string ThemeButtonLabel
        {
            get
            {
                bool isAr = LocalizationService.Instance.IsArabic;
                if (_isDarkMode)
                {
                    return isAr ? "☀️ الوضع النهاري" : "☀️ Day Mode";
                }
                return isAr ? "🌙 الوضع الليلي" : "🌙 Night Mode";
            }
        }

        public string CurrentThemeStatusText
        {
            get
            {
                bool isAr = LocalizationService.Instance.IsArabic;
                if (_isDarkMode)
                {
                    return isAr ? "الوضع الحالي: ليلي 🌙" : "Current Theme: Night Mode 🌙";
                }
                return isAr ? "الوضع الحالي: نهاري ☀️" : "Current Theme: Day Mode ☀️";
            }
        }

        static ThemeService()
        {
            try
            {
                EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(ContextMenu), ContextMenu.OpenedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(OnPopupMenuOpened));
                EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(ToolTip), ToolTip.OpenedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(OnPopupMenuOpened));
            }
            catch
            {
                // Safe guard in non-standard hosts
            }
        }

        private static void OnDatePickerCalendarOpened(object? sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (sender is DatePicker dp)
            {
                var popup = dp.Template?.FindName("PART_Popup", dp) as Popup;
                if (popup?.Child != null)
                {
                    Instance.ApplyThemeToSubtree(popup.Child);
                }
            }
        }

        private static void OnComboBoxDropDownOpened(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (sender is ComboBox cmb)
            {
                var popup = cmb.Template?.FindName("PART_Popup", cmb) as Popup;
                if (popup?.Child != null)
                {
                    Instance.ApplyThemeToSubtree(popup.Child);
                }
            }
        }

        private static void OnPopupMenuOpened(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (sender is DependencyObject dep)
            {
                Instance.ApplyThemeToSubtree(dep);
            }
        }

        private static readonly DependencyProperty OriginalBackgroundProperty =
            DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("OriginalBackground", typeof(SolidColorBrush), typeof(ThemeService), new PropertyMetadata(null));
        private static readonly DependencyProperty HasOriginalBackgroundProperty =
            DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("HasOriginalBackground", typeof(bool), typeof(ThemeService), new PropertyMetadata(false));

        private static readonly DependencyProperty OriginalForegroundProperty =
            DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("OriginalForeground", typeof(SolidColorBrush), typeof(ThemeService), new PropertyMetadata(null));
        private static readonly DependencyProperty HasOriginalForegroundProperty =
            DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("HasOriginalForeground", typeof(bool), typeof(ThemeService), new PropertyMetadata(false));

        private static readonly DependencyProperty OriginalBorderBrushProperty =
            DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("OriginalBorderBrush", typeof(SolidColorBrush), typeof(ThemeService), new PropertyMetadata(null));
        private static readonly DependencyProperty HasOriginalBorderBrushProperty =
            DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("HasOriginalBorderBrush", typeof(bool), typeof(ThemeService), new PropertyMetadata(false));

        public async Task LoadSettingsAsync()
        {
            bool isDark = false;
            try
            {
                using var db = new ArchiveDbContext();
                var settings = await db.SystemSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();
                if (settings != null && string.Equals(settings.SelectedTheme, "Dark", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    isDark = true;
                }
            }
            catch
            {
            }

            SetTheme(isDark, saveToDb: false);
        }

        public void ToggleTheme()
        {
            SetTheme(!_isDarkMode, saveToDb: true);
        }

        public void SetTheme(bool isDark, bool saveToDb = true)
        {
            _isDarkMode = isDark;
            UpdateResourcePalette(isDark);
            NotifyThemeBindings();

            if (Application.Current?.Windows != null)
            {
                foreach (Window window in Application.Current.Windows)
                {
                    ApplyThemeToWindow(window);
                }
            }

            if (saveToDb)
            {
                _ = SaveThemePreferenceAsync(isDark ? "Dark" : "Light");
            }
        }

        public void NotifyThemeBindings()
        {
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(nameof(IsDarkMode)));
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(nameof(ThemeIcon)));
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(nameof(ThemeButtonLabel)));
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(nameof(CurrentThemeStatusText)));
        }

        private static async Task SaveThemePreferenceAsync(string themeName)
        {
            try
            {
                using var db = new ArchiveDbContext();
                var settings = await db.SystemSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();
                if (settings != null)
                {
                    settings.SelectedTheme = themeName;
                    settings.UpdatedAt = DateTime.Now;
                    await db.SaveChangesAsync();
                }
            }
            catch
            {
            }
        }

        private static void UpdateResourcePalette(bool isDark)
        {
            if (Application.Current == null) return;
            var res = Application.Current.Resources;

            if (isDark)
            {
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "PrimaryColor", "PrimaryBrush", "#3B82F6");
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "PrimaryHoverColor", "PrimaryHoverBrush", "#2563EB");
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "PrimaryLightColor", "PrimaryLightBrush", "#1E3A8A");
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "AccentColor", "AccentBrush", "#14B8A6");

                SetColorAndBrush(res, "BgDarkSidebar", "SidebarBrush", "#090E1A");
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "BgSidebarHover", "SidebarHoverBrush", "#1E293B");
                SetBrush(res, "SidebarCardBrush", "#111827");
                SetBrush(res, "SidebarNavBtnBgBrush", "#111827");
                SetBrush(res, "SidebarNavBtnFgBrush", "#F1F5F9");
                SetBrush(res, "SidebarNavBtnBorderBrush", "#334155");
                SetBrush(res, "SidebarNavBtnHoverBrush", "#1E293B");
                SetBrush(res, "HeaderBannerBgBrush", "#111827");

                SetColorAndBrush(res, "BgApp", "AppBackgroundBrush", "#0B1120");
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "BgCard", "CardBrush", "#1E293B");
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "BorderLight", "BorderBrushLight", "#334155");
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "TextPrimary", "TextPrimaryBrush", "#F8FAFC");
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "TextSecondary", "TextSecondaryBrush", "#94A3B8");

                SetBrush(res, "SurfaceSubtleBrush", "#0F172A");
                SetBrush(res, "InputBackgroundBrush", "#0F172A");
                SetBrush(res, "InputForegroundBrush", "#F8FAFC");
                SetBrush(res, "SecondaryButtonBgBrush", "#1E293B");
                SetBrush(res, "SecondaryButtonFgBrush", "#F1F5F9");
                SetBrush(res, "SecondaryButtonHoverBrush", "#334155");

                SetBrush(res, "PopupBgBrush", "#1E293B");
                SetBrush(res, "PopupHoverBrush", "#334155");
                SetBrush(res, "CalendarHeaderBgBrush", "#0F172A");
                SetBrush(res, "DatePickerBtnBgBrush", "#1E293B");
                SetBrush(res, "DatePickerBtnHoverBrush", "#334155");

                SetBrush(res, "DataGridHeaderBgBrush", "#0F172A");
                SetBrush(res, "DataGridRowHoverBrush", "#283548");
                SetBrush(res, "DataGridGridLinesBrush", "#334155");
            }
            else
            {
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "PrimaryColor", "PrimaryBrush", "#1A56DB");
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "PrimaryHoverColor", "PrimaryHoverBrush", "#1E429F");
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "PrimaryLightColor", "PrimaryLightBrush", "#E1EFFE");
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "AccentColor", "AccentBrush", "#0D9488");

                SetColorAndBrush(res, "BgDarkSidebar", "SidebarBrush", "#0F172A");
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "BgSidebarHover", "SidebarHoverBrush", "#1E293B");
                SetBrush(res, "SidebarCardBrush", "#1E293B");
                SetBrush(res, "SidebarNavBtnBgBrush", "#1E293B");
                SetBrush(res, "SidebarNavBtnFgBrush", "#FFFFFF");
                SetBrush(res, "SidebarNavBtnBorderBrush", "#334155");
                SetBrush(res, "SidebarNavBtnHoverBrush", "#334155");
                SetBrush(res, "HeaderBannerBgBrush", "#1E293B");

                SetColorAndBrush(res, "BgApp", "AppBackgroundBrush", "#F8FAFC");
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "BgCard", "CardBrush", "#FFFFFF");
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "BorderLight", "BorderBrushLight", "#E2E8F0");
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "TextPrimary", "TextPrimaryBrush", "#0F172A");
                SetColorAndBrush(res, "TextSecondary", "TextSecondaryBrush", "#64748B");

                SetBrush(res, "SurfaceSubtleBrush", "#F8FAFC");
                SetBrush(res, "InputBackgroundBrush", "#FFFFFF");
                SetBrush(res, "InputForegroundBrush", "#0F172A");
                SetBrush(res, "SecondaryButtonBgBrush", "#FFFFFF");
                SetBrush(res, "SecondaryButtonFgBrush", "#0F172A");
                SetBrush(res, "SecondaryButtonHoverBrush", "#F1F5F9");

                SetBrush(res, "PopupBgBrush", "#FFFFFF");
                SetBrush(res, "PopupHoverBrush", "#E1EFFE");
                SetBrush(res, "CalendarHeaderBgBrush", "#F1F5F9");
                SetBrush(res, "DatePickerBtnBgBrush", "#F1F5F9");
                SetBrush(res, "DatePickerBtnHoverBrush", "#E2E8F0");

                SetBrush(res, "DataGridHeaderBgBrush", "#F8FAFC");
                SetBrush(res, "DataGridRowHoverBrush", "#F8FAFC");
                SetBrush(res, "DataGridGridLinesBrush", "#F1F5F9");
            }
        }

        private static void SetColorAndBrush(ResourceDictionary res, string colorKey, string brushKey, string hex)
        {
            var c = (Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(hex);
            res[colorKey] = c;
            var b = new SolidColorBrush(c);
            b.Freeze();
            res[brushKey] = b;
        }

        private static void SetBrush(ResourceDictionary res, string brushKey, string hex)
        {
            var c = (Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(hex);
            var b = new SolidColorBrush(c);
            b.Freeze();
            res[brushKey] = b;
        }

        public void ApplyThemeToWindow(Window? window)
        {
            if (window == null) return;

            ApplyThemeRecursive(window, _isDarkMode, new HashSet<DependencyObject>());

            window.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Loaded, new Action(() =>
            {
                ApplyThemeRecursive(window, _isDarkMode, new HashSet<DependencyObject>());
            }));
        }

        public void ApplyThemeToSubtree(DependencyObject root)
        {
            if (root == null) return;
            ApplyThemeRecursive(root, _isDarkMode, new HashSet<DependencyObject>());
        }

        private static bool IsTemplateLeafControl(DependencyObject node)
        {
            return node is ButtonBase ||
                   node is ComboBox ||
                   node is ComboBoxItem ||
                   node is DatePicker ||
                   node is DatePickerTextBox ||
                   node is Calendar ||
                   node is CalendarItem ||
                   node is CalendarDayButton ||
                   node is CalendarButton ||
                   node is TextBox ||
                   node is PasswordBox ||
                   node is ScrollBar ||
                   node is FlowDocumentScrollViewer;
        }

        private void ApplyThemeRecursive(DependencyObject node, bool isDark, HashSet<DependencyObject> visited)
        {
            if (node == null || !visited.Add(node)) return;

            // Keep WYSIWYG white print paper preview untouched
            if (node is FlowDocumentScrollViewer)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (node is FrameworkElement fe)
            {
                ThemeElement(fe, isDark);
            }

            // Always walk logical children (covers Button content StackPanels, ListBoxItems, etc.)
            foreach (var child in LogicalTreeHelper.GetChildren(node))
            {
                if (child is DependencyObject depChild)
                {
                    ApplyThemeRecursive(depChild, isDark, visited);
                }
            }

            // Do NOT walk into visual template internals of leaf/templated controls;
            // their ControlTemplates use {DynamicResource} and {TemplateBinding} directly.
            if (IsTemplateLeafControl(node))
            {
                return;
            }

            int visualCount = (node is Visual || node is System.Windows.Media.Media3D.Visual3D)
                ? VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(node)
                : 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < visualCount; i++)
            {
                var vChild = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(node, i);
                ApplyThemeRecursive(vChild, isDark, visited);
            }
        }

        private static void ThemeElement(FrameworkElement fe, bool isDark)
        {
            if (fe is Window win)
            {
                ThemeProperty(win, Control.BackgroundProperty, HasOriginalBackgroundProperty, OriginalBackgroundProperty, DarkBackgroundMap, isDark);
                return;
            }

            if (fe is Border border)
            {
                ThemeProperty(border, Border.BackgroundProperty, HasOriginalBackgroundProperty, OriginalBackgroundProperty, DarkBackgroundMap, isDark);
                ThemeProperty(border, Border.BorderBrushProperty, HasOriginalBorderBrushProperty, OriginalBorderBrushProperty, DarkBorderMap, isDark);
                return;
            }

            if (fe is TextBlock tb)
            {
                ThemeProperty(tb, TextBlock.ForegroundProperty, HasOriginalForegroundProperty, OriginalForegroundProperty, DarkForegroundMap, isDark);
                return;
            }

            if (fe is TextBox txt)
            {
                ThemeLocalInputOverrides(txt, isDark);
                txt.CaretBrush = GetDynamicOrFallbackBrush("InputForegroundBrush", isDark ? "#F8FAFC" : "#0F172A");
                return;
            }

            if (fe is PasswordBox pwd)
            {
                ThemeLocalInputOverrides(pwd, isDark);
                pwd.CaretBrush = GetDynamicOrFallbackBrush("InputForegroundBrush", isDark ? "#F8FAFC" : "#0F172A");
                return;
            }

            if (fe is DatePicker dp)
            {
                dp.CalendarOpened -= OnDatePickerCalendarOpened;
                dp.CalendarOpened += OnDatePickerCalendarOpened;
                ThemeLocalInputOverrides(dp, isDark);
                return;
            }

            if (fe is ComboBox cmb)
            {
                cmb.DropDownOpened -= OnComboBoxDropDownOpened;
                cmb.DropDownOpened += OnComboBoxDropDownOpened;
                ThemeLocalInputOverrides(cmb, isDark);
                return;
            }

            if (fe is ListBox)
            {
                ThemeLocalInputOverrides((Control)fe, isDark);
                return;
            }

            if (fe is Control ctrl)
            {
                ThemeProperty(ctrl, Control.BackgroundProperty, HasOriginalBackgroundProperty, OriginalBackgroundProperty, DarkBackgroundMap, isDark);
                ThemeProperty(ctrl, Control.ForegroundProperty, HasOriginalForegroundProperty, OriginalForegroundProperty, DarkForegroundMap, isDark);
                ThemeProperty(ctrl, Control.BorderBrushProperty, HasOriginalBorderBrushProperty, OriginalBorderBrushProperty, DarkBorderMap, isDark);
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
