                        _loadedFlowDoc = DocumentPrintExportService.LoadWordFlowDocument(_decryptedBytes, _extension, _originalFileName);
                        WordDocViewer.Document = _loadedFlowDoc;
                    }
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    Loc.IsArabic ? $"تعذر عرض الملف المؤرشف:\n{ex.Message}" : $"Failed to preview archived file:\n{ex.Message}",
                    Loc["Error"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
            }
        }

        private static bool IsImageExtension(string ext)
        {
            return ext == ".png" || ext == ".jpg" || ext == ".jpeg" || ext == ".bmp" || ext == ".gif" || ext == ".tiff" || ext == ".tif";
        }

        private static bool IsTextExtension(string ext)
        {
            return ext == ".txt" || ext == ".csv" || ext == ".json" || ext == ".xml" || ext == ".log" || ext == ".md";
        }

        private void CreateTempSessionFile()
        {
            if (_tempViewingFilePath != null && File.Exists(_tempViewingFilePath)) return;

            string tempDir = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "DocuArchive_SecureSession");
            if (!Directory.Exists(tempDir)) Directory.CreateDirectory(tempDir);

            _tempViewingFilePath = Path.Combine(tempDir, $"View_{Guid.NewGuid():N}{_extension}");
            File.WriteAllBytes(_tempViewingFilePath, _decryptedBytes);
        }

        private void BtnOpenTempProtected_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                CreateTempSessionFile();
                if (_tempViewingFilePath != null && File.Exists(_tempViewingFilePath))
                {
                    Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo
                    {
                        FileName = _tempViewingFilePath,
                        UseShellExecute = true
                    });
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    Loc.IsArabic ? $"تعذر فتح العرض المؤقت: {ex.Message}" : $"Unable to open protected session: {ex.Message}",
                    Loc["Warning"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
            }
        }

        private void BtnZoomIn_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            _zoomFactor = Math.Min(4.0, _zoomFactor + 0.25);
            ImgScale.ScaleX = _zoomFactor;
            ImgScale.ScaleY = _zoomFactor;
        }

        private void BtnZoomOut_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            _zoomFactor = Math.Max(0.25, _zoomFactor - 0.25);
            ImgScale.ScaleX = _zoomFactor;
            ImgScale.ScaleY = _zoomFactor;
        }

        private void BtnZoomReset_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            _zoomFactor = 1.0;
            ImgScale.ScaleX = 1.0;
            ImgScale.ScaleY = 1.0;
        }

        private FlowDocument BuildTextFlowDocument()
        {
            return new FlowDocument(new Paragraph(new Run(TxtDecryptedContent.Text)))
            {
                Background = Brushes.White,
                Foreground = Brushes.Black,
                FlowDirection = Loc.CurrentFlowDirection,
                PagePadding = new Thickness(40),
                FontFamily = Application.Current?.Resources["AppFontFamily"] as FontFamily ?? new FontFamily("Segoe UI, Tahoma, Arial")
            };
        }

        private void BtnPrintAttachment_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            var printDlg = new PrintDialog();
            if (printDlg.ShowDialog() != true) return;

            try
            {
                // 1. PDF Attachment: preserve exact PDF bytes or send PDF to physical printer
                if (_extension == ".pdf")
                {
                    if (DocumentPrintExportService.IsVirtualPdfPrinter(printDlg.PrintQueue))
                    {
                        DocumentPrintExportService.SavePdfFromBytesFileDialog(this, _decryptedBytes, _originalFileName);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        CreateTempSessionFile();
                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_tempViewingFilePath))
                        {
                            DocumentPrintExportService.PrintExistingPdfToPhysicalPrinter(printDlg, _tempViewingFilePath);
                        }
                    }
                    return;
                }

                // 2. Image Attachment
                if (_loadedBitmap != null)
                {
                    if (DocumentPrintExportService.IsVirtualPdfPrinter(printDlg.PrintQueue))
                    {
                        DocumentPrintExportService.SaveBitmapToPdfFileDialog(this, _loadedBitmap, _originalFileName);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        var vis = new DrawingVisual();
                        using (var dc = vis.RenderOpen())
                        {
                            double pageW = printDlg.PrintableAreaWidth;
                            double pageH = printDlg.PrintableAreaHeight;
                            double margin = 30;
                            Rect rect = new Rect(margin, margin, Math.Max(100, pageW - margin * 2), Math.Max(100, pageH - margin * 2));
                            dc.DrawImage(_loadedBitmap, rect);
                        }
                        string safeTitle = DocumentPrintExportService.SanitizePrintJobTitle($"{(Loc.IsArabic ? "طباعة مرفق" : "Print Attachment")} - {_originalFileName}");
                        printDlg.PrintVisual(vis, safeTitle);
                    }
                    return;
                }

                // 3. Word (.docx / .doc / .rtf / .odt / .html) Attachment
                if (_loadedFlowDoc != null)
                {
                    DocumentPrintExportService.PrintOrExportFlowDocument(
                        printDlg,
                        _loadedFlowDoc,
                        _originalFileName,
                        this,
                        freshDocFactory: () => DocumentPrintExportService.LoadWordFlowDocument(_decryptedBytes, _extension, _originalFileName),
                        rawWordBytes: _decryptedBytes,
                        wordExt: _extension);
                    return;
                }

                // 4. Plain Text Attachment
                var textDoc = BuildTextFlowDocument();
                DocumentPrintExportService.PrintOrExportFlowDocument(
                    printDlg,
                    textDoc,
                    _originalFileName,
                    this,
                    freshDocFactory: BuildTextFlowDocument);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    Loc.IsArabic ? $"تعذر تنفيذ الطباعة أو الحفظ:\n{ex.Message}" : $"Failed to print or export:\n{ex.Message}",
                    Loc["Error"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
            }
        }

        private void BtnClose_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            Close();
        }

        private void AttachmentViewerDialog_Closed(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                PdfWebBrowser.Navigate("about:blank");

                // Clean up stale temporary files (>30 mins) without breaking active print spooler jobs
                string tempDir = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "DocuArchive_SecureSession");
                if (Directory.Exists(tempDir))
                {
                    DateTime cutoff = DateTime.UtcNow.AddMinutes(-30);
                    foreach (string file in Directory.GetFiles(tempDir))
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            if (File.GetLastWriteTimeUtc(file) < cutoff)
                            {
                                File.Delete(file);
                            }
                        }
                        catch { }
                    }
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                // Ignore if process still holds lock briefly
            }
        }
    }
}
Models\CorrespondentEntity.cs
Saved inbound/outbound correspondent entity model (name, scope, notes) with bilingual display and digit conversion.
نموذج جهة الصادر/الوارد المحفوظة (الاسم، النوع، الملاحظات) مع عرض ثنائي اللغة وتحويل الأرقام.
using System;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations.Schema;
using DocuArchivePro.Services;

namespace DocuArchivePro.Models
{
    public class CorrespondentEntity
    {
        [Key]
        public int Id { get; set; }

        [Required]
        [MaxLength(250)]
        public string Name { get; set; } = string.Empty;

        [MaxLength(50)]
        public string EntityType { get; set; } = "Both"; // "Both", "Inbound", "Outbound"

        [MaxLength(500)]
        public string Notes { get; set; } = string.Empty;

        public bool IsActive { get; set; } = true;

        public DateTime CreatedAt { get; set; } = DateTime.Now;

        [NotMapped]
        public string DisplayName
        {
            get
            {
                bool isAr = LocalizationService.Instance.IsArabic;
                int sep = Name.IndexOf(" / ", StringComparison.Ordinal);
                if (sep > 0)
                {
                    string part1 = Name.Substring(0, sep).Trim();
                    string part2 = Name.Substring(sep + 3).Trim();
                    string chosen = isAr ? part1 : part2;
                    return DateTimeHelper.FormatDigits(chosen, isAr);
                }
                return DateTimeHelper.FormatDigits(Name, isAr);
            }
        }

        [NotMapped]
        public string LocalizedEntityType
        {
            get
            {
                bool isAr = LocalizationService.Instance.IsArabic;
                return EntityType switch
                {
                    "Inbound" => isAr ? "جهة وارد" : "Inbound Entity",
                    "Outbound" => isAr ? "جهة صادر" : "Outbound Entity",
                    _ => isAr ? "وارد وصادر معاً" : "Inbound & Outbound"
                };
            }
        }

        [NotMapped]
        public string DisplayNotes => DateTimeHelper.FormatDigits(Notes, LocalizationService.Instance.IsArabic);
    }
}
Services\SecurityService.cs
Users, permissions and security settings service with VerifyActionCodeAsync (dossier PIN) and VerifyEntityCodeAsync (dedicated entity password).
خدمة المستخدمين والصلاحيات وإعدادات الأمان، وتتضمن VerifyActionCodeAsync لرمز الأضابير وVerifyEntityCodeAsync لكلمة سر إضافة الجهات.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using DocuArchivePro.Data;
using DocuArchivePro.Models;

namespace DocuArchivePro.Services
{
    public class SecurityService : ISecurityService
    {
        private static SecurityService? _instance;
        public static SecurityService Instance => _instance ??= new SecurityService();

        public AppUser CurrentUser { get; set; } = new AppUser
        {
            Username = "SuperUser",
            RoleTitle = "مدير النظام العام - كامل الصلاحيات",
            LoginPassword = "Admin",
            IsAdmin = true,
            CanAdd = true,
            CanEdit = true,
            CanDelete = true,
            CanScan = true,
            CanPrint = true,
            CanManageStorage = true,
            CanManageEntities = true
        };

        public async Task<List<AppUser>> GetAllUsersAsync()
        {
            using var db = new ArchiveDbContext();
            return await db.Users.OrderByDescending(u => u.IsAdmin).ThenBy(u => u.Id).ToListAsync();
        }

        public async Task<AppUser> SaveUserAsync(AppUser user)
        {
            using var db = new ArchiveDbContext();
            if (user.IsAdmin)
            {
                user.CanAdd = true;
                user.CanEdit = true;
                user.CanDelete = true;
                user.CanScan = true;
                user.CanPrint = true;
                user.CanManageStorage = true;
                user.CanManageEntities = true;
            }

            if (user.Id > 0)
            {
                var existing = await db.Users.FindAsync(user.Id);
                if (existing != null)
                {
                    existing.Username = user.Username;
                    existing.RoleTitle = user.RoleTitle;
                    existing.LoginPassword = user.LoginPassword;
                    existing.IsAdmin = user.IsAdmin;
                    existing.CanAdd = user.CanAdd;
                    existing.CanEdit = user.CanEdit;
                    existing.CanDelete = user.CanDelete;
                    existing.CanScan = user.CanScan;
                    existing.CanPrint = user.CanPrint;
                    existing.CanManageStorage = user.CanManageStorage;
                    existing.CanManageEntities = user.CanManageEntities;
                    await db.SaveChangesAsync();
                    if (CurrentUser.Id == existing.Id)
                    {
                        CurrentUser = existing;
                    }
                    return existing;
                }
            }

            user.CreatedAt = DateTime.Now;
            await db.Users.AddAsync(user);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            return user;
        }

        public async Task<bool> DeleteUserAsync(int userId)
        {
            using var db = new ArchiveDbContext();
            int totalUsers = await db.Users.CountAsync();
            if (totalUsers <= 1)
            {
                return false; // Prevent deleting the last remaining user
            }

            var user = await db.Users.FindAsync(userId);
            if (user == null) return false;

            db.Users.Remove(user);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            return true;
        }

        public async Task<SystemSettings> GetSettingsAsync()
        {
            using var db = new ArchiveDbContext();
            var settings = await db.SystemSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();
            if (settings == null)
            {
                string defaultVault = Path.Combine(
                    Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData),
                    "DocuArchivePro",
                    "Vault");

                settings = new SystemSettings
                {
                    ActionSecurityCode = "1234",
                    EntitySecurityCode = "5678",
                    VaultStoragePath = defaultVault,
                    DefaultScannerDeviceId = "WINDOWS_INTERACTIVE_WIA_DIALOG",
                    DefaultScannerName = "نافذة اختيار السكانر القياسية في الويندوز (Windows WIA / TWAIN Dialog)",
                    DefaultScanDpi = 300,
                    DefaultScanColorMode = 1,
                    UseScannerFeeder = false,
                    UpdatedAt = DateTime.Now
                };
                await db.SystemSettings.AddAsync(settings);
                await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            return settings;
        }

        public async Task<SystemSettings> SaveSettingsAsync(SystemSettings settings)
        {
            using var db = new ArchiveDbContext();
            var existing = await db.SystemSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();
            if (existing == null)
            {
                settings.UpdatedAt = DateTime.Now;
                await db.SystemSettings.AddAsync(settings);
                await db.SaveChangesAsync();
                return settings;
            }

            existing.ActionSecurityCode = settings.ActionSecurityCode;
            existing.EntitySecurityCode = settings.EntitySecurityCode;
            existing.VaultStoragePath = settings.VaultStoragePath;
            existing.DefaultScannerDeviceId = settings.DefaultScannerDeviceId;
            existing.DefaultScannerName = settings.DefaultScannerName;
            existing.DefaultScanDpi = settings.DefaultScanDpi;
            existing.DefaultScanColorMode = settings.DefaultScanColorMode;
            existing.UseScannerFeeder = settings.UseScannerFeeder;
            existing.UpdatedAt = DateTime.Now;

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            return existing;
        }

        public async Task<bool> VerifyActionCodeAsync(string enteredCode)
        {
            var settings = await GetSettingsAsync();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(settings.ActionSecurityCode))
            {
                return true;
            }
            return string.Equals(enteredCode?.Trim(), settings.ActionSecurityCode.Trim(), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        }

        public async Task<bool> VerifyEntityCodeAsync(string enteredCode)
        {
            var settings = await GetSettingsAsync();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(settings.EntitySecurityCode))
            {
                return true;
            }
            return string.Equals(enteredCode?.Trim(), settings.EntitySecurityCode.Trim(), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        }
    }
}
Views\ActionPinDialog.xaml.cs
PIN dialog supporting two modes: dossier/sensitive-action PIN and the dedicated entity-addition password.
نافذة التحقق من كلمة السر وتدعم وضعين: رمز الأضابير والإجراءات الحساسة، وكلمة سر إضافة جهات الصادر والوارد.
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Input;
using DocuArchivePro.Services;

namespace DocuArchivePro.Views
{
    public partial class ActionPinDialog : Window
    {
        private readonly bool _isEntityPinMode;
        public ILocalizationService Loc => LocalizationService.Instance;

        public ActionPinDialog(string actionDescription, bool isEntityPinMode = false)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _isEntityPinMode = isEntityPinMode;
            DataContext = this;
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(actionDescription))
            {
                TxtActionDescription.Text = actionDescription;
            }
            if (_isEntityPinMode)
            {
                TxtHeader.Text = Loc["EntityPinDialogHeader"];
                TxtPrompt.Text = Loc["EntityPinDialogPrompt"];
            }
            Loaded += (s, e) => PwdActionCode.Focus();
        }

        private async void BtnConfirm_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            await VerifyAndCloseAsync();
        }

        private async void PwdActionCode_KeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.Key == Key.Enter)
            {
                await VerifyAndCloseAsync();
            }
        }

        private async Task VerifyAndCloseAsync()
        {
            string entered = PwdActionCode.Password;
            bool isValid = _isEntityPinMode
                ? await SecurityService.Instance.VerifyEntityCodeAsync(entered)
                : await SecurityService.Instance.VerifyActionCodeAsync(entered);
            if (isValid)
            {
                DialogResult = true;
                Close();
            }
            else
            {
                TxtError.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
                PwdActionCode.Clear();
                PwdActionCode.Focus();
            }
        }

        private void BtnCancel_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            DialogResult = false;
            Close();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Prompts for the dedicated pre-configured Entity Addition Security Password when a user lacks entity permission.
        /// </summary>
        public static bool VerifyEntityAdditionPin(string actionTitle, Window? owner = null)
        {
            var pinDlg = new ActionPinDialog(actionTitle, isEntityPinMode: true)
            {
                Owner = owner ?? Application.Current.MainWindow
            };
            return pinDlg.ShowDialog() == true;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Shows the warning message first; if the user selects Yes, prompts for the pre-configured Action Security Code.
        /// </summary>
        public static bool ConfirmWarningAndVerifyPin(string actionTitle, string warningMessage, Window? owner = null)
        {
            bool isAr = LocalizationService.Instance.IsArabic;
            string caption = isAr ? $"تنبيه تحذيري - {actionTitle}" : $"Security Warning - {actionTitle}";

            var warningResult = MessageBox.Show(
                warningMessage,
                caption,
                MessageBoxButton.YesNo,
                MessageBoxImage.Warning);

            if (warningResult != MessageBoxResult.Yes)
            {
                return false;
            }

            var pinDlg = new ActionPinDialog(actionTitle)
            {
                Owner = owner ?? Application.Current.MainWindow
            };

            return pinDlg.ShowDialog() == true;
        }
    }
}
Views\AddEntityDialog.xaml.cs
Dialog for adding/editing a saved correspondent entity and persisting it to the database.
نافذة إضافة وتعديل جهة صادر/وارد محفوظة وحفظها في قاعدة البيانات.
using System;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using DocuArchivePro.Models;
using DocuArchivePro.Services;

namespace DocuArchivePro.Views
{
    public partial class AddEntityDialog : Window, INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        private readonly IDocumentService _documentService;
        private readonly CorrespondentEntity? _existingEntity;
        private readonly PropertyChangedEventHandler _locHandler;

        public ILocalizationService Loc => LocalizationService.Instance;
        public CorrespondentEntity? CreatedEntity { get; private set; }
        public bool IsEditMode => _existingEntity != null;

        public string DialogTitle => Loc[IsEditMode ? "EditEntityWindowTitle" : "AddEntityWindowTitle"];
        public string HeaderText => Loc[IsEditMode ? "EditEntityHeader" : "AddEntityHeader"];
        public string SubheaderText => Loc["AddEntitySubheader"];
        public string SaveButtonText => Loc["AddEntitySaveBtn"];
        public string HeaderIconPath => (SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser.IsAdmin || SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser.CanManageEntities)
            ? "pack://application:,,,/Resources/Icons/AppIcon.png"
            : "pack://application:,,,/Resources/Icons/SecurityLockIcon.png";

        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;

        public AddEntityDialog(
            IDocumentService documentService,
            CorrespondentEntity? existingEntity = null,
            string? initialName = null,
            string? defaultEntityType = null)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _documentService = documentService;
            _existingEntity = existingEntity;
            DataContext = this;

            if (_existingEntity != null)
            {
                TxtEntityName.Text = _existingEntity.Name;
                TxtEntityNotes.Text = _existingEntity.Notes;
                SelectEntityTypeTag(_existingEntity.EntityType);
            }
            else
            {
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(initialName))
                {
                    TxtEntityName.Text = initialName.Trim();
                }
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(defaultEntityType))
                {
                    SelectEntityTypeTag(defaultEntityType);
                }
            }

            _locHandler = (s, e) =>
            {
                if (e.PropertyName == nameof(ILocalizationService.CurrentLanguage))
                {
                    PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(nameof(DialogTitle)));
                    PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(nameof(HeaderText)));
                    PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(nameof(SubheaderText)));
                    PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(nameof(SaveButtonText)));
                }
            };
            Loc.PropertyChanged += _locHandler;
            Closed += (s, e) => Loc.PropertyChanged -= _locHandler;

            Loaded += (s, e) =>
            {
                TxtEntityName.Focus();
                TxtEntityName.SelectAll();
            };
        }

        private void SelectEntityTypeTag(string entityType)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < CmbEntityType.Items.Count; i++)
            {
                if (CmbEntityType.Items[i] is ComboBoxItem item &&
                    string.Equals(item.Tag?.ToString(), entityType, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    CmbEntityType.SelectedIndex = i;
                    return;
                }
            }
            CmbEntityType.SelectedIndex = 0;
        }

        private async void BtnSave_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            string name = TxtEntityName.Text.Trim();
            string notes = TxtEntityNotes.Text.Trim();
            string entityType = (CmbEntityType.SelectedItem as ComboBoxItem)?.Tag?.ToString() ?? "Both";

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name))
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    Loc.IsArabic ? "يرجى إدخال اسم جهة الصادر أو الوارد." : "Please enter the correspondent entity name.",
                    Loc["Warning"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                TxtEntityName.Focus();
                return;
            }

            try
            {
                if (_existingEntity != null)
                {
                    _existingEntity.Name = name;
                    _existingEntity.EntityType = entityType;
                    _existingEntity.Notes = notes;
                    CreatedEntity = await _documentService.UpdateCorrespondentEntityAsync(_existingEntity);
                }
                else
                {
                    var newEntity = new CorrespondentEntity
                    {
                        Name = name,
                        EntityType = entityType,
                        Notes = notes,
                        IsActive = true
                    };
                    CreatedEntity = await _documentService.AddCorrespondentEntityAsync(newEntity);
                }

                DialogResult = true;
                Close();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    Loc.IsArabic ? $"فشل حفظ الجهة:\n{ex.Message}" : $"Failed to save entity:\n{ex.Message}",
                    Loc["Error"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
            }
        }

        private void BtnCancel_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            DialogResult = false;
            Close();
        }
    }
}
