Views\AboutDialog.xaml.cs
Code-behind for AboutDialog applying active localization fonts and Day/Night themes on load.
منطق تشغيل شاشة حول هذا البرنامج (AboutDialog) مع تطبيق الخطوط والثيم الليلي/النهاري تلقائياً.
using System.Windows;
using DocuArchivePro.Services;

namespace DocuArchivePro.Views
{
    public partial class AboutDialog : Window
    {
        public ILocalizationService Loc => LocalizationService.Instance;

        public AboutDialog()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            DataContext = this;

            Loaded += (s, e) =>
            {
                FontService.Instance.ApplyToWindow(this);
                ThemeService.Instance.ApplyThemeToWindow(this);
            };
        }

        private void BtnClose_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            Close();
        }
    }
}
ViewModels\DocumentEntryViewModel.cs
Document entry ViewModel: saved entity lists, manual entry, real WIA scanner command, and immediate PreviewPendingFileCommand (👁) before saving.
نموذج عرض إدخال المعاملة: قوائم الجهات المحفوظة، الإدخال اليدوي، المسح الضوئي الحقيقي WIA، وأمر المعاينة الفورية للملفات المرفقة قبل الحفظ (👁).
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Input;
using Microsoft.Win32;
using DocuArchivePro.Models;
using DocuArchivePro.Services;
using DocuArchivePro.Views;

namespace DocuArchivePro.ViewModels
{
    public class DocumentEntryViewModel : ViewModelBase
    {
        private readonly IDocumentService _documentService;
        private readonly IScannerService _scannerService;
        private readonly ILocalizationService _loc;
        private readonly IFileStorageService _fileStorageService;

        public ILocalizationService Loc => _loc;

        private int _id;
        private string _registrationNumber = string.Empty;
        private DocumentType _type = DocumentType.Inbound;
        private DateTime _registrationDate = DateTime.Now;
        private DateTime _documentDate = DateTime.Now;
        private string _subject = string.Empty;
        private string _sender = string.Empty;
        private string _recipient = string.Empty;
        private int? _categoryId;
        private DocumentPriority _priority = DocumentPriority.Normal;
        private DocumentStatus _status = DocumentStatus.New;
        private string _deliveryMethod = LocalizationService.Instance.IsArabic ? "تسليم يدوي" : "Hand Delivery";
        private string _externalReferenceNumber = string.Empty;
        private DateTime? _followUpDate;
        private string _notes = string.Empty;
        private string _department = string.Empty;
        private string _tags = string.Empty;

        public bool IsEditMode => _id > 0;
        public string DialogTitle => IsEditMode 
            ? $"{_loc["Edit"]} - {_registrationNumber}" 
            : _loc["NewRegistration"];

        public int Id { get => _id; set => SetField(ref _id, value); }
        public string RegistrationNumber { get => _registrationNumber; set => SetField(ref _registrationNumber, value); }
        public DocumentType Type
        {
            get => _type;
            set
            {
                if (SetField(ref _type, value))
                {
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsInbound));
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsOutbound));
                    UpdateAutoRegNumber();
                }
            }
        }

        public bool IsInbound
        {
            get => Type == DocumentType.Inbound;
            set { if (value) Type = DocumentType.Inbound; }
        }

        public bool IsOutbound
        {
            get => Type == DocumentType.Outbound;
            set { if (value) Type = DocumentType.Outbound; }
        }

        public DateTime RegistrationDate { get => _registrationDate; set => SetField(ref _registrationDate, value); }
        public DateTime DocumentDate { get => _documentDate; set => SetField(ref _documentDate, value); }
        public string Subject { get => _subject; set => SetField(ref _subject, value); }
        public string Sender
        {
            get => _sender;
            set
            {
                if (SetField(ref _sender, value))
                {
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SelectedSenderEntityName));
                }
            }
        }
        public string Recipient
        {
            get => _recipient;
            set
            {
                if (SetField(ref _recipient, value))
                {
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SelectedRecipientEntityName));
                }
            }
        }

        public bool CanManualEntityEntry => SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser.IsAdmin || SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser.CanManageEntities;
        public Visibility EntityLockIconVisibility => CanManualEntityEntry ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;

        public string? SelectedSenderEntityName
        {
            get => string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_sender) ? null : SavedEntityNames.FirstOrDefault(n => string.Equals(n, _sender, StringComparison.Ordinal));
            set
            {
                if (value != null && !string.Equals(_sender, value, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                {
                    Sender = value;
                }
            }
        }

        public string? SelectedRecipientEntityName
        {
            get => string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_recipient) ? null : SavedEntityNames.FirstOrDefault(n => string.Equals(n, _recipient, StringComparison.Ordinal));
            set
            {
                if (value != null && !string.Equals(_recipient, value, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                {
                    Recipient = value;
                }
            }
        }
        public int? CategoryId { get => _categoryId; set => SetField(ref _categoryId, value); }
        public DocumentPriority Priority
        {
            get => _priority;
            set
            {
                if (SetField(ref _priority, value))
                {
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(PriorityIndex));
                }
            }
        }

        public int PriorityIndex
        {
            get => Math.Max(0, (int)Priority - 1);
            set => Priority = (DocumentPriority)(value + 1);
        }

        public DocumentStatus Status
        {
            get => _status;
            set
            {
                if (SetField(ref _status, value))
                {
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusIndex));
                }
            }
        }

        public int StatusIndex
        {
            get => Math.Max(0, (int)Status - 1);
            set => Status = (DocumentStatus)(value + 1);
        }
        public string DeliveryMethod { get => _deliveryMethod; set => SetField(ref _deliveryMethod, value); }
        public string ExternalReferenceNumber { get => _externalReferenceNumber; set => SetField(ref _externalReferenceNumber, value); }
        public DateTime? FollowUpDate { get => _followUpDate; set => SetField(ref _followUpDate, value); }
        public string Notes { get => _notes; set => SetField(ref _notes, value); }
        public string Department { get => _department; set => SetField(ref _department, value); }
        public string Tags { get => _tags; set => SetField(ref _tags, value); }

        public IDocumentService DocumentService => _documentService;

        public ObservableCollection<Category> Categories { get; } = new();
        public ObservableCollection<CorrespondentEntity> CorrespondentEntities { get; } = new();
        public ObservableCollection<string> SavedEntityNames { get; } = new();
        public ObservableCollection<DocumentAttachment> ExistingAttachments { get; } = new();
        public ObservableCollection<string> PendingFilePaths { get; } = new();

        public ICommand SaveCommand { get; }
        public ICommand CancelCommand { get; }
        public ICommand BrowseFileCommand { get; }
        public ICommand ScanFileCommand { get; }
        public ICommand PreviewPendingFileCommand { get; }
        public ICommand RemovePendingFileCommand { get; }
        public ICommand DeleteExistingAttachmentCommand { get; }
        public ICommand OpenAttachmentCommand { get; }
        public ICommand AddNewCategoryCommand { get; }
        public ICommand EditSelectedCategoryCommand { get; }
        public ICommand AddSenderEntityCommand { get; }
        public ICommand AddRecipientEntityCommand { get; }

        public event Action<bool>? RequestClose;
        public event Action? RequestOpenAddCategoryDialog;
        public event Action<Category>? RequestOpenEditCategoryDialog;
        public event Action<string, string>? RequestOpenAddEntityDialog;

        public DocumentEntryViewModel(
            IDocumentService documentService, 
            IScannerService scannerService, 
            IFileStorageService fileStorageService,
            ILocalizationService localizationService,
            Document? existingDocument = null,
            DocumentType initialType = DocumentType.Inbound)
        {
            _documentService = documentService;
            _scannerService = scannerService;
            _fileStorageService = fileStorageService;
            _loc = localizationService;

            SaveCommand = new RelayCommand(async () => await SaveAsync());
            CancelCommand = new RelayCommand(() => RequestClose?.Invoke(false));
            BrowseFileCommand = new RelayCommand(BrowseFile);
            ScanFileCommand = new RelayCommand(async () => await ScanDocumentAsync());
            PreviewPendingFileCommand = new RelayCommand(p => PreviewPendingFile(p as string));
            RemovePendingFileCommand = new RelayCommand(p => { if (p is string s) PendingFilePaths.Remove(s); });
            DeleteExistingAttachmentCommand = new RelayCommand(async p => { if (p is DocumentAttachment att) await DeleteExistingAttachmentAsync(att); });
            OpenAttachmentCommand = new RelayCommand(p => { if (p is DocumentAttachment att) _fileStorageService.OpenAttachmentInApp(att); });
            AddNewCategoryCommand = new RelayCommand(() => RequestOpenAddCategoryDialog?.Invoke());
            AddSenderEntityCommand = new RelayCommand(() => RequestOpenAddEntityDialog?.Invoke("Sender", Sender));
            AddRecipientEntityCommand = new RelayCommand(() => RequestOpenAddEntityDialog?.Invoke("Recipient", Recipient));
            EditSelectedCategoryCommand = new RelayCommand(() =>
            {
                var selectedCat = Categories.FirstOrDefault(c => c.Id == CategoryId);
                if (selectedCat == null)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        _loc.IsArabic ? "يرجى اختيار إضبارة من القائمة لتعديلها." : "Please select a dossier from the list to edit.",
                        _loc["Warning"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
                    return;
                }
                RequestOpenEditCategoryDialog?.Invoke(selectedCat);
            });

            LoadCategories();
            LoadEntities();

            if (existingDocument != null)
            {
                Id = existingDocument.Id;
                RegistrationNumber = existingDocument.RegistrationNumber;
                Type = existingDocument.Type;
                RegistrationDate = existingDocument.RegistrationDate;
                DocumentDate = existingDocument.DocumentDate;
                Subject = existingDocument.Subject;
                Sender = existingDocument.Sender;
                Recipient = existingDocument.Recipient;
                CategoryId = existingDocument.CategoryId;
                Priority = existingDocument.Priority;
                Status = existingDocument.Status;
                DeliveryMethod = existingDocument.DeliveryMethod;
                ExternalReferenceNumber = existingDocument.ExternalReferenceNumber;
                FollowUpDate = existingDocument.FollowUpDate;
                Notes = existingDocument.Notes;
                Department = existingDocument.Department;
                Tags = existingDocument.Tags;

                if (existingDocument.Attachments != null)
                {
                    foreach (var a in existingDocument.Attachments) ExistingAttachments.Add(a);
                }
            }
            else
            {
                Type = initialType;
                UpdateAutoRegNumber();
            }
        }

        private async void LoadCategories()
        {
            var cats = await _documentService.GetCategoriesAsync();
            Categories.Clear();
            foreach (var c in cats) Categories.Add(c);
            if (!CategoryId.HasValue && Categories.Count > 0)
            {
                CategoryId = Categories[0].Id;
            }
        }

        public async Task AddAndSelectCategoryAsync(Category newCategory)
        {
            var cats = await _documentService.GetCategoriesAsync();
            Categories.Clear();
            foreach (var c in cats) Categories.Add(c);
            CategoryId = newCategory.Id;
        }

        private async void LoadEntities()
        {
            await RefreshSavedEntitiesAsync();
        }

        public async Task RefreshSavedEntitiesAsync()
        {
            string preservedSender = _sender;
            string preservedRecipient = _recipient;

            var entities = await _documentService.GetCorrespondentEntitiesAsync();
            CorrespondentEntities.Clear();
            SavedEntityNames.Clear();

            foreach (var e in entities)
            {
                CorrespondentEntities.Add(e);
                string display = e.DisplayName;
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(display) && !SavedEntityNames.Contains(display))
                {
                    SavedEntityNames.Add(display);
                }
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(preservedSender) && !SavedEntityNames.Contains(preservedSender))
            {
                SavedEntityNames.Add(preservedSender);
            }
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(preservedRecipient) && !SavedEntityNames.Contains(preservedRecipient))
            {
                SavedEntityNames.Add(preservedRecipient);
            }

            _sender = preservedSender;
            _recipient = preservedRecipient;
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(Sender));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(Recipient));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SelectedSenderEntityName));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SelectedRecipientEntityName));
        }

        public async Task AddAndSelectEntityAsync(CorrespondentEntity newEntity, string targetField)
        {
            string selectedName = newEntity.DisplayName;
            if (string.Equals(targetField, "Recipient", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                Recipient = selectedName;
            }
            else
            {
                Sender = selectedName;
            }

            await RefreshSavedEntitiesAsync();
        }

        private async void UpdateAutoRegNumber()
        {
            if (!IsEditMode)
            {
                RegistrationNumber = await _documentService.GenerateNextRegistrationNumberAsync(Type);
            }
        }

        private void BrowseFile()
        {
            var dlg = new OpenFileDialog
            {
                Title = _loc["SelectFile"],
                Filter = "All Supported Files (*.pdf;*.jpg;*.png;*.docx;*.xlsx)|*.pdf;*.jpg;*.jpeg;*.png;*.docx;*.xlsx;*.tiff|PDF Documents (*.pdf)|*.pdf|Images (*.jpg;*.png)|*.jpg;*.jpeg;*.png|Office Docs (*.docx;*.xlsx)|*.docx;*.xlsx|All Files (*.*)|*.*",
                Multiselect = true
            };

            if (dlg.ShowDialog() == true)
            {
                foreach (var file in dlg.FileNames)
                {
                    if (!PendingFilePaths.Contains(file))
                    {
                        PendingFilePaths.Add(file);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        private async Task ScanDocumentAsync()
        {
            var user = SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser;
            if (!user.IsAdmin && !user.CanScan)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    _loc.IsArabic ? "عذراً، المستخدم الحالي لا يملك صلاحية استخدام الماسح الضوئي." : "Sorry, the current user does not have scanner permission.",
                    _loc.IsArabic ? "صلاحيات المستخدم" : "User Permissions",
                    MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                return;
            }

            try
            {
                var dlg = new ScannerDialog(_scannerService)
                {
                    Owner = Application.Current.MainWindow
                };

                if (dlg.ShowDialog() == true && dlg.ScannedResultBytes != null)
                {
                    string tempPath = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"Scan_{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd_HHmmss}.png");
                    await File.WriteAllBytesAsync(tempPath, dlg.ScannedResultBytes);

                    PendingFilePaths.Add(tempPath);
                    MessageBox.Show(_loc["ScanComplete"], _loc["Success"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"{_loc["Error"]}: {ex.Message}", _loc["Error"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
            }
        }

        private void PreviewPendingFile(string? filePath)
        {
            try
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(filePath) || !File.Exists(filePath))
                {
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        _loc.IsArabic ? "الملف المحدد غير موجود على الجهاز." : "The selected file could not be found.",
                        _loc["Warning"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                    return;
                }

                byte[] rawBytes = File.ReadAllBytes(filePath);
                byte[] decrypted = FileStorageService.DecryptBytes(rawBytes);

                var viewer = new AttachmentViewerDialog(decrypted, Path.GetFileName(filePath))
                {
                    Owner = Application.Current.Windows.OfType<Window>().FirstOrDefault(w => w.IsActive) ?? Application.Current.MainWindow
                };
                viewer.ShowDialog();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    _loc.IsArabic ? $"تعذر استعراض الملف:\n{ex.Message}" : $"Failed to preview file:\n{ex.Message}",
                    _loc["Error"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
            }
        }

        private async Task DeleteExistingAttachmentAsync(DocumentAttachment att)
        {
            var user = SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser;
            if (!user.IsAdmin && !user.CanDelete)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    _loc.IsArabic ? "عذراً، المستخدم الحالي لا يملك صلاحية حذف المرفقات المؤرشفة." : "Sorry, the current user does not have permission to delete archived attachments.",
                    _loc.IsArabic ? "صلاحيات المستخدم" : "User Permissions",
                    MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                return;
            }

            string pinTitle = _loc.IsArabic ? "حذف مرفق مؤرشف ومشفر" : "Delete Encrypted Attachment";
            string pinMsg = _loc.IsArabic
                ? $"هل أنت متأكد من رغبتك في حذف هذا الملف المؤرشف نهائياً؟\n\nاسم الملف: {att.FileName}"
                : $"Are you sure you want to permanently delete this archived file?\n\nFilename: {att.FileName}";

            if (ActionPinDialog.ConfirmWarningAndVerifyPin(pinTitle, pinMsg))
            {
                await _documentService.DeleteAttachmentAsync(att.Id);
                ExistingAttachments.Remove(att);
            }
        }

        private async Task SaveAsync()
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Subject))
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    _loc.IsArabic ? "يرجى إدخال موضوع المعاملة." : "Please enter the document subject.",
                    _loc["Error"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                return;
            }

            var doc = new Document
            {
                Id = Id,
                RegistrationNumber = RegistrationNumber,
                Type = Type,
                RegistrationDate = RegistrationDate,
                DocumentDate = DocumentDate,
                Subject = Subject,
                Sender = Sender,
                Recipient = Recipient,
                CategoryId = CategoryId,
                Priority = Priority,
                Status = Status,
                DeliveryMethod = DeliveryMethod,
                ExternalReferenceNumber = ExternalReferenceNumber,
                FollowUpDate = FollowUpDate,
                Notes = Notes,
                Department = Department,
                Tags = Tags
            };

            try
            {
                if (IsEditMode)
                {
                    await _documentService.UpdateDocumentAsync(doc);
                    // Add any newly attached files in edit mode
                    foreach (var path in PendingFilePaths)
                    {
                        await _documentService.AddAttachmentAsync(doc.Id, path);
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    await _documentService.CreateDocumentAsync(doc, PendingFilePaths.ToList());
                }

                MessageBox.Show(_loc["SavedSuccessfully"], _loc["Success"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
                RequestClose?.Invoke(true);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"{_loc["Error"]}: {ex.Message}", _loc["Error"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
            }
        }
    }
}
ViewModels\MainViewModel.cs
Main workspace ViewModel coordinating filters, statistics, RBAC permission checks, PIN prompts, dossier wizard triggers, and backup import.
نموذج العرض الرئيسي الذي يربط التبويبات، الفلاتر، التحقق من صلاحيات المستخدمين، تعديل وحذف المعاملات والأضابير، واستيراد النسخ الاحتياطية.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Data;
using System.Windows.Input;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using DocuArchivePro.Data;
using DocuArchivePro.Models;
using DocuArchivePro.Services;
using DocuArchivePro.Views;

namespace DocuArchivePro.ViewModels
{
    public class MainViewModel : ViewModelBase
    {
        private readonly IDocumentService _documentService;
        private readonly IScannerService _scannerService;
        private readonly IFileStorageService _fileStorageService;
        private readonly ILocalizationService _loc;
        private readonly DispatcherTimer _clockTimer;

        public ILocalizationService Loc => _loc;

        // Live Time, Day, Gregorian & Hijri Date Properties
        private string _liveClockText = string.Empty;
        public string LiveClockText { get => _liveClockText; set => SetField(ref _liveClockText, value); }

        private string _liveDayText = string.Empty;
        public string LiveDayText { get => _liveDayText; set => SetField(ref _liveDayText, value); }

        private string _liveGregorianText = string.Empty;
        public string LiveGregorianText { get => _liveGregorianText; set => SetField(ref _liveGregorianText, value); }

        private string _liveHijriText = string.Empty;
        public string LiveHijriText { get => _liveHijriText; set => SetField(ref _liveHijriText, value); }

        private string _currentUserSummary = string.Empty;
        public string CurrentUserSummary { get => _currentUserSummary; set => SetField(ref _currentUserSummary, value); }

        private string _currentVaultPathText = string.Empty;
        public string CurrentVaultPathText { get => _currentVaultPathText; set => SetField(ref _currentVaultPathText, value); }

        // Dynamic UI & Button Scale Factor synced with LocalizationService.UiScale
        public double UiScale
        {
            get => _loc.UiScale;
            set
            {
                _loc.UiScale = value;
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(UiScale));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(UiScalePercentText));
            }
        }

        public string UiScalePercentText => _loc.UiScalePercentText;

        // Active Navigation Tab: "Dashboard", "Inbound", "Outbound", "All", "Settings"
        private string _currentTab = "Dashboard";
        public string CurrentTab
        {
            get => _currentTab;
            set
            {
                if (SetField(ref _currentTab, value))
                {
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsDashboardTab));
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsInboundTab));
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsOutboundTab));
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsAllDocsTab));
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsSettingsTab));
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsDocumentsViewVisible));
                    ExecuteTabChange();
                }
            }
        }

        public bool IsDashboardTab => CurrentTab == "Dashboard";
        public bool IsInboundTab => CurrentTab == "Inbound";
        public bool IsOutboundTab => CurrentTab == "Outbound";
        public bool IsAllDocsTab => CurrentTab == "All";
        public bool IsSettingsTab => CurrentTab == "Settings";
        public bool IsDocumentsViewVisible => !IsSettingsTab;

        // Statistics
        private DashboardStatistics _stats = new();
        public DashboardStatistics Stats { get => _stats; set => SetField(ref _stats, value); }

        // Documents Lists
        public ObservableCollection<Document> Documents { get; } = new();
        public ObservableCollection<Category> Categories { get; } = new();
        public ObservableCollection<CorrespondentEntity> CorrespondentEntities { get; } = new();

        private Document? _selectedDocument;
        public Document? SelectedDocument
        {
            get => _selectedDocument;
            set => SetField(ref _selectedDocument, value);
        }

        // Filters
        private string _searchQuery = string.Empty;
        public string SearchQuery
        {
            get => _searchQuery;
            set
            {
                if (SetField(ref _searchQuery, value))
                {
                    _ = RefreshDocumentsAsync();
                }
            }
        }

        private DateTime? _filterFromDate;
        public DateTime? FilterFromDate
        {
            get => _filterFromDate;
            set { if (SetField(ref _filterFromDate, value)) _ = RefreshDocumentsAsync(); }
        }

        private DateTime? _filterToDate;
        public DateTime? FilterToDate
        {
            get => _filterToDate;
            set { if (SetField(ref _filterToDate, value)) _ = RefreshDocumentsAsync(); }
        }

        private int? _filterCategoryId;
        public int? FilterCategoryId
        {
            get => _filterCategoryId;
            set { if (SetField(ref _filterCategoryId, value)) _ = RefreshDocumentsAsync(); }
        }

        private DocumentPriority? _filterPriority;
        public DocumentPriority? FilterPriority
        {
            get => _filterPriority;
            set { if (SetField(ref _filterPriority, value)) _ = RefreshDocumentsAsync(); }
        }

        // Commands
        public ICommand NavigateTabCommand { get; }
        public ICommand NewDocumentCommand { get; }
        public ICommand EditDocumentCommand { get; }
        public ICommand DeleteDocumentCommand { get; }
        public ICommand PreviewSelectedAttachmentCommand { get; }
        public ICommand PrintSelectedDocumentCommand { get; }
        public ICommand OpenPrintCenterCommand { get; }
        public ICommand RefreshCommand { get; }
        public ICommand ResetFiltersCommand { get; }
        public ICommand OpenAttachmentCommand { get; }
        public ICommand OpenVaultCommand { get; }
        public ICommand OpenDatabaseFolderCommand { get; }
        public ICommand ChangeLanguageCommand { get; }
        public ICommand QuickScanCommand { get; }
        public ICommand OpenUserManagementCommand { get; }
        public ICommand OpenCalendarDialogCommand { get; }
        public ICommand OpenAboutDialogCommand { get; }
        public ICommand ZoomInUiCommand { get; }
        public ICommand ZoomOutUiCommand { get; }
        public ICommand ResetUiZoomCommand { get; }
        public ICommand OpenAddCategoryCommand { get; }
        public ICommand EditCategoryCommand { get; }
        public ICommand DeleteCategoryCommand { get; }
        public ICommand ImportCategoryBackupCommand { get; }
        public ICommand OpenAddEntityCommand { get; }
        public ICommand EditEntityCommand { get; }
        public ICommand DeleteEntityCommand { get; }
        public ICommand ToggleThemeCommand { get; }
        public ICommand SetThemeCommand { get; }

        public IReadOnlyList<ArabicFontItem> AvailableArabicFonts => FontService.Instance.AvailableArabicFonts;
        private ArabicFontItem? _selectedArabicFont;
        public ArabicFontItem? SelectedArabicFont
        {
            get => _selectedArabicFont ?? FontService.Instance.CurrentArabicFont;
            set
            {
                if (SetField(ref _selectedArabicFont, value) && value != null)
                {
                    _ = FontService.Instance.SaveSelectedFontAsync(value.Key);
                }
            }
        }

        public bool IsArabicLanguage => _loc.IsArabic;

        public event Action<Document?, DocumentType>? RequestOpenDocumentDialog;
        public event Action? RequestOpenScannerDialog;
        public event Action? RequestOpenVaultSettingsDialog;
        public event Action<Document?, int>? RequestOpenPrintDialog;
        public event Action? RequestOpenUserLoginDialog;
        public event Action? RequestOpenCalendarDialog;
        public event Action? RequestOpenAboutDialog;
        public event Action? RequestOpenAddCategoryDialog;
        public event Action<Category>? RequestOpenEditCategoryDialog;
        public event Action<Category>? RequestOpenDeleteCategoryWizard;
        public event Action? RequestImportCategoryBackup;
        public event Action? RequestOpenAddEntityDialog;
        public event Action<CorrespondentEntity>? RequestOpenEditEntityDialog;

        public MainViewModel(
            IDocumentService documentService,
            IScannerService scannerService,
            IFileStorageService fileStorageService,
            ILocalizationService localizationService)
        {
            _documentService = documentService;
            _scannerService = scannerService;
            _fileStorageService = fileStorageService;
            _loc = localizationService;

            _loc.PropertyChanged += (s, e) =>
            {
                if (e.PropertyName == nameof(ILocalizationService.CurrentLanguage))
                {
                    OnLanguageUpdated();
                }
                else if (e.PropertyName == nameof(ILocalizationService.UiScale) || e.PropertyName == nameof(ILocalizationService.UiScalePercentText))
                {
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(UiScale));
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(UiScalePercentText));
                }
            };

            UpdateClockAndUserBanner();
            _clockTimer = new DispatcherTimer { Interval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1) };
            _clockTimer.Tick += (s, e) => UpdateClockAndUserBanner();
            _clockTimer.Start();

            NavigateTabCommand = new RelayCommand(tab => CurrentTab = tab?.ToString() ?? "Dashboard");

            NewDocumentCommand = new RelayCommand(param =>
            {
                var user = SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser;
                if (!user.IsAdmin && !user.CanAdd)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        _loc.IsArabic
                            ? "عذراً، المستخدم الحالي لا يملك صلاحية إضافة معاملات جديدة."
                            : "Sorry, the current user does not have permission to add new documents.",
                        _loc.IsArabic ? "صلاحيات المستخدم" : "User Permissions",
                        MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                    return;
                }

                DocumentType type = DocumentType.Inbound;
                if (param?.ToString() == "Outbound" || IsOutboundTab) type = DocumentType.Outbound;
                RequestOpenDocumentDialog?.Invoke(null, type);
            });

            EditDocumentCommand = new RelayCommand(() =>
            {
                if (SelectedDocument == null) return;

                var user = SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser;
                if (!user.IsAdmin && !user.CanEdit)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        _loc.IsArabic
                            ? "عذراً، المستخدم الحالي لا يملك صلاحية تعديل المعاملات المؤرشفة."
                            : "Sorry, the current user does not have permission to edit archived documents.",
                        _loc.IsArabic ? "صلاحيات المستخدم" : "User Permissions",
                        MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                    return;
                }

                string regNum = DateTimeHelper.FormatDigits(SelectedDocument.RegistrationNumber, _loc.IsArabic);
                string title = _loc.IsArabic ? "تعديل معاملة مؤرشفة" : "Edit Archived Document";
                string msg = _loc.IsArabic
                    ? $"هل أنت متأكد من رغبتك في إجراء تعديل على هذه المعاملة المؤرشفة؟\n\nرقم القيد: {regNum}\nالموضوع: {SelectedDocument.Subject}"
                    : $"Are you sure you want to edit this archived document?\n\nRegistration No.: {regNum}\nSubject: {SelectedDocument.Subject}";

                if (ActionPinDialog.ConfirmWarningAndVerifyPin(title, msg))
                {
                    RequestOpenDocumentDialog?.Invoke(SelectedDocument, SelectedDocument.Type);
                }
            }, () => SelectedDocument != null);

            DeleteDocumentCommand = new RelayCommand(async () => await DeleteSelectedDocumentAsync(), () => SelectedDocument != null);

            PreviewSelectedAttachmentCommand = new RelayCommand(() =>
            {
                if (SelectedDocument == null) return;
                if (SelectedDocument.Attachments != null && SelectedDocument.Attachments.Count > 0)
                {
                    var firstAtt = SelectedDocument.Attachments.First();
                    _fileStorageService.OpenAttachmentInApp(firstAtt);
                }
                else
                {
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        _loc.IsArabic
                            ? "لا توجد ملفات أو صور مرفقة بهذه المعاملة حالياً.\nيمكنك إرفاق ملف أو مسحه ضوئياً من خلال تعديل المعاملة."
                            : "No files or images are currently attached to this document.\nYou can attach or scan a file by editing the document.",
                        _loc.IsArabic ? "استعراض المرفقات المشفرة" : "Encrypted Attachments Viewer",
                        MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
                }
            }, () => SelectedDocument != null);

            PrintSelectedDocumentCommand = new RelayCommand(() =>
            {
                var user = SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser;
                if (!user.IsAdmin && !user.CanPrint)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        _loc.IsArabic
                            ? "عذراً، المستخدم الحالي لا يملك صلاحية الطباعة."
                            : "Sorry, the current user does not have printing permission.",
                        _loc.IsArabic ? "صلاحيات المستخدم" : "User Permissions",
                        MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                    return;
                }
                if (SelectedDocument != null)
                {
                    RequestOpenPrintDialog?.Invoke(SelectedDocument, 4); // Single Document Card
                }
            }, () => SelectedDocument != null);

            OpenPrintCenterCommand = new RelayCommand(() =>
            {
                var user = SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser;
                if (!user.IsAdmin && !user.CanPrint)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        _loc.IsArabic
                            ? "عذراً، المستخدم الحالي لا يملك صلاحية الطباعة واستخراج التقارير."
                            : "Sorry, the current user does not have permission to print or export reports.",
                        _loc.IsArabic ? "صلاحيات المستخدم" : "User Permissions",
                        MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                    return;
                }
                RequestOpenPrintDialog?.Invoke(SelectedDocument, 0);
            });

            RefreshCommand = new RelayCommand(async () => await LoadDataAsync());

            ResetFiltersCommand = new RelayCommand(() =>
            {
                _searchQuery = string.Empty;
                _filterFromDate = null;
                _filterToDate = null;
                _filterCategoryId = null;
                _filterPriority = null;
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SearchQuery));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(FilterFromDate));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(FilterToDate));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(FilterCategoryId));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(FilterPriority));
                _ = RefreshDocumentsAsync();
            });

            OpenAttachmentCommand = new RelayCommand(param =>
            {
                if (param is DocumentAttachment att)
                {
                    _fileStorageService.OpenAttachmentInApp(att);
                }
            });

            OpenVaultCommand = new RelayCommand(() =>
            {
                var user = SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser;
                if (!user.IsAdmin && !user.CanManageStorage)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        _loc.IsArabic
                            ? "عذراً، المستخدم الحالي لا يملك صلاحية إدارة مكان حفظ وتشفير المستودع."
                            : "Sorry, the current user does not have permission to manage the encrypted vault location.",
                        _loc.IsArabic ? "صلاحيات المستخدم" : "User Permissions",
                        MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                    return;
                }
                RequestOpenVaultSettingsDialog?.Invoke();
            });

            OpenDatabaseFolderCommand = new RelayCommand(() =>
            {
                string dir = Path.GetDirectoryName(ArchiveDbContext.DatabasePath)!;
                Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo { FileName = dir, UseShellExecute = true });
            });

            ChangeLanguageCommand = new RelayCommand(langCode =>
            {
                if (langCode is string code)
                {
                    _loc.SetLanguage(code);
                }
            });

            OpenAddCategoryCommand = new RelayCommand(() => RequestOpenAddCategoryDialog?.Invoke());

            EditCategoryCommand = new RelayCommand(param =>
            {
                if (param is Category cat)
                {
                    RequestOpenEditCategoryDialog?.Invoke(cat);
                }
            });

            ImportCategoryBackupCommand = new RelayCommand(() => RequestImportCategoryBackup?.Invoke());

            OpenAddEntityCommand = new RelayCommand(() =>
            {
                var user = SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser;
                if (!user.IsAdmin && !user.CanManageEntities)
                {
                    string actionTitle = _loc.IsArabic
                        ? "إضافة جهة صادر / وارد للمحفوظات (كلمة سر الجهات الخاصة)"
                        : "Add Saved Inbound / Outbound Entity (Entity Security Password)";
                    if (!ActionPinDialog.VerifyEntityAdditionPin(actionTitle))
                    {
                        return;
                    }
                }
                RequestOpenAddEntityDialog?.Invoke();
            });

            EditEntityCommand = new RelayCommand(param =>
            {
                if (param is CorrespondentEntity entity)
                {
                    var user = SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser;
                    if (!user.IsAdmin && !user.CanManageEntities)
                    {
                        string actionTitle = _loc.IsArabic
                            ? "تعديل جهة صادر / وارد محفوظة (كلمة سر الجهات الخاصة)"
                            : "Edit Saved Correspondent Entity (Entity Security Password)";
                        if (!ActionPinDialog.VerifyEntityAdditionPin(actionTitle))
                        {
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                    RequestOpenEditEntityDialog?.Invoke(entity);
                }
            });

            DeleteEntityCommand = new RelayCommand(async param =>
            {
                if (param is CorrespondentEntity entity)
                {
                    var user = SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser;
                    if (!user.IsAdmin && !user.CanManageEntities)
                    {
                        string actionTitle = _loc.IsArabic
                            ? "حذف جهة صادر / وارد محفوظة (كلمة سر الجهات الخاصة)"
                            : "Delete Saved Correspondent Entity (Entity Security Password)";
                        if (!ActionPinDialog.VerifyEntityAdditionPin(actionTitle))
                        {
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        var confirm = MessageBox.Show(
                            _loc.IsArabic
                                ? $"هل أنت متأكد من حذف الجهة المحفوظة ({entity.DisplayName}) من القائمة؟"
                                : $"Are you sure you want to remove the saved entity ({entity.DisplayName})?",
                            _loc["ConfirmDelete"],
                            MessageBoxButton.YesNo,
                            MessageBoxImage.Question);
                        if (confirm != MessageBoxResult.Yes) return;
                    }

                    await _documentService.DeleteCorrespondentEntityAsync(entity.Id);
                    await LoadDataAsync();
                }
            });

            DeleteCategoryCommand = new RelayCommand(param =>
            {
                if (param is Category cat)
                {
                    var user = SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser;
                    if (!user.IsAdmin && !user.CanDelete)
                    {
                        MessageBox.Show(
                            _loc.IsArabic
                                ? "عذراً، المستخدم الحالي لا يملك صلاحية حذف الأضابير والمعاملات."
                                : "Sorry, the current user does not have permission to delete dossiers or documents.",
                            _loc.IsArabic ? "صلاحيات المستخدم" : "User Permissions",
                            MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                        return;
                    }

                    string prompt = _loc.IsArabic
                        ? $"هل أنت متأكد من رغبتك في البدء بإجراءات حذف الإضبارة / التصنيف:\n\n{cat.DisplayName}؟\n\nيتطلب المتابعة إدخال كلمة سر الحذف المعدة مسبقاً."
                        : $"Are you sure you want to start deletion procedures for this dossier / category:\n\n{cat.DisplayName}?\n\nContinuing requires the pre-configured deletion security password.";
                    string title = _loc.IsArabic ? "حذف إضبارة / تصنيف" : "Delete Dossier / Category";

                    if (ActionPinDialog.ConfirmWarningAndVerifyPin(title, prompt))
                    {
                        RequestOpenDeleteCategoryWizard?.Invoke(cat);
                    }
                }
            });

            QuickScanCommand = new RelayCommand(() =>
            {
                var user = SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser;
                if (!user.IsAdmin && !user.CanScan)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        _loc.IsArabic
                            ? "عذراً، المستخدم الحالي لا يملك صلاحية استخدام السكانر."
                            : "Sorry, the current user does not have scanner permission.",
                        _loc.IsArabic ? "صلاحيات المستخدم" : "User Permissions",
                        MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                    return;
                }
                RequestOpenScannerDialog?.Invoke();
            });

            OpenUserManagementCommand = new RelayCommand(() => RequestOpenUserLoginDialog?.Invoke());
            OpenCalendarDialogCommand = new RelayCommand(() => RequestOpenCalendarDialog?.Invoke());
            OpenAboutDialogCommand = new RelayCommand(() => RequestOpenAboutDialog?.Invoke());
            ZoomInUiCommand = new RelayCommand(() => UiScale += 0.08);
            ZoomOutUiCommand = new RelayCommand(() => UiScale -= 0.08);
            ResetUiZoomCommand = new RelayCommand(() => UiScale = 1.0);
            ToggleThemeCommand = new RelayCommand(() => _loc.ToggleTheme());
            SetThemeCommand = new RelayCommand(param =>
            {
                bool isDark = string.Equals(param?.ToString(), "Dark", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                _loc.SetTheme(isDark);
            });
        }

        private void OnLanguageUpdated()
        {
            UpdateClockAndUserBanner();
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsArabicLanguage));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(UiScalePercentText));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(Stats));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(AvailableArabicFonts));
            CollectionViewSource.GetDefaultView(Documents)?.Refresh();
            CollectionViewSource.GetDefaultView(Categories)?.Refresh();
            CollectionViewSource.GetDefaultView(CorrespondentEntities)?.Refresh();
        }

        public void UpdateClockAndUserBanner()
        {
            var now = DateTime.Now;
            bool isAr = _loc.IsArabic;

            if (isAr)
            {
                LiveClockText = DateTimeHelper.GetTimeArabic(now);
                LiveDayText = DateTimeHelper.GetDayNameArabic(now);
                LiveGregorianText = DateTimeHelper.GetGregorianDateArabic(now);
                LiveHijriText = DateTimeHelper.GetHijriDateArabic(now);
            }
            else
            {
                LiveClockText = DateTimeHelper.GetTimeEnglish(now);
                LiveDayText = DateTimeHelper.GetDayNameEnglish(now);
                LiveGregorianText = DateTimeHelper.GetGregorianDateEnglish(now);
                LiveHijriText = DateTimeHelper.GetHijriDateEnglish(now);
            }

            var u = SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser;
            CurrentUserSummary = $"{u.LocalizedUsername} ({u.LocalizedRoleTitle})";
            CurrentVaultPathText = _fileStorageService.StorageRootDirectory;
        }

        public async Task LoadDataAsync()
        {
            UpdateClockAndUserBanner();
            Stats = await _documentService.GetDashboardStatisticsAsync();
            var cats = await _documentService.GetCategoriesAsync();
            Categories.Clear();
            foreach (var c in cats) Categories.Add(c);
            var entities = await _documentService.GetCorrespondentEntitiesAsync();
            CorrespondentEntities.Clear();
            foreach (var ent in entities) CorrespondentEntities.Add(ent);
            await RefreshDocumentsAsync();
        }

        private async void ExecuteTabChange()
        {
            await RefreshDocumentsAsync();
            ThemeService.Instance.ApplyThemeToWindow(Application.Current?.MainWindow);
        }

        public async Task RefreshDocumentsAsync()
        {
            DocumentType? docType = null;
            if (IsInboundTab) docType = DocumentType.Inbound;
            else if (IsOutboundTab) docType = DocumentType.Outbound;

            var criteria = new DocumentFilterCriteria
            {
                Type = docType,
                FromDate = FilterFromDate,
                ToDate = FilterToDate,
                CategoryId = FilterCategoryId,
                Priority = FilterPriority,
                SearchQuery = SearchQuery
            };

            var docs = await _documentService.SearchDocumentsAsync(criteria);
            Documents.Clear();
            foreach (var d in docs) Documents.Add(d);

            Stats = await _documentService.GetDashboardStatisticsAsync();
        }

        private async Task DeleteSelectedDocumentAsync()
        {
            if (SelectedDocument == null) return;

            var user = SecurityService.Instance.CurrentUser;
            if (!user.IsAdmin && !user.CanDelete)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    _loc.IsArabic
                        ? "عذراً، المستخدم الحالي لا يملك صلاحية حذف المعاملات المؤرشفة."
                        : "Sorry, the current user does not have permission to delete archived documents.",
                    _loc.IsArabic ? "صلاحيات المستخدم" : "User Permissions",
                    MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                return;
            }

            string regNum = DateTimeHelper.FormatDigits(SelectedDocument.RegistrationNumber, _loc.IsArabic);
            string warningMsg = $"{_loc["DeleteMessage"]}\n\n{_loc["RegistrationNumber"]}: {regNum}\n{_loc["Subject"]}: {SelectedDocument.Subject}";
            string pinTitle = _loc.IsArabic ? "حذف معاملة مؤرشفة نهائياً" : "Permanently Delete Archived Document";

            if (ActionPinDialog.ConfirmWarningAndVerifyPin(pinTitle, warningMsg))
            {
                await _documentService.DeleteDocumentAsync(SelectedDocument.Id);
                await LoadDataAsync();
            }
        }
    }
}
Services\WiaScannerService.cs
Real Windows WIA hardware scanner service executing COM acquisition on a dedicated STA thread with automatic ADF configuration, .NET 8 System.__ComObject interop, and localized hardware error messages.
خدمة المسح الضوئي الحقيقي عبر Windows WIA على خيط STA مخصص مع دعم الساحب الآلي ADF تلقائياً وتوافق .NET 8 مع كائنات COM ورسائل خطأ مترجمة بدون ماسح افتراضي.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.Win32;

namespace DocuArchivePro.Services
{
    public class WiaScannerService : IScannerService
    {
        // WIA Format GUIDs
        private const string WiaFormatBMP = "{B96B3CAB-0728-11D3-9D7B-0000F81EF32E}";
        private const string WiaFormatPNG = "{B96B3CAF-0728-11D3-9D7B-0000F81EF32E}";
        private const string WiaFormatJPEG = "{B96B3CAE-0728-11D3-9D7B-0000F81EF32E}";
        private const string WiaFormatTIFF = "{B96B3CB1-0728-11D3-9D7B-0000F81EF32E}";

        // Common WIA HResult codes
        private const int WIA_ERROR_PAPER_EMPTY = unchecked((int)0x80210003);
        private const int WIA_ERROR_OFFLINE = unchecked((int)0x80210005);
        private const int WIA_ERROR_BUSY = unchecked((int)0x80210006);
        private const int WIA_S_NO_DEVICE_AVAILABLE = unchecked((int)0x80210015);
        private const int WIA_ERROR_USER_CANCELLED = unchecked((int)0x80210064);

        public const string InteractiveDialogDeviceId = "WINDOWS_INTERACTIVE_WIA_DIALOG";

        public bool IsWiaAvailable()
        {
            try
            {
                var type = Type.GetTypeFromProgID("WIA.DeviceManager");
                return type != null;
            }
            catch
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        public Task<List<ScannerDeviceInfo>> GetAvailableScannersAsync()
        {
            return Task.Run(() =>
            {
                bool isAr = LocalizationService.Instance.IsArabic;
                var list = new List<ScannerDeviceInfo>();

                // 1. Enumerate active WIA Scanners and Imaging Devices
                try
                {
                    var mgrType = Type.GetTypeFromProgID("WIA.DeviceManager");
                    if (mgrType != null)
                    {
                        dynamic? deviceManagerObj = Activator.CreateInstance(mgrType);
                        if (deviceManagerObj is not null)
                        {
                            dynamic deviceManager = deviceManagerObj;
                            foreach (dynamic info in deviceManager.DeviceInfos)
                            {
                                try
                                {
                                    string id = (string)info.DeviceID;
                                    string name = GetWiaPropertyString(info.Properties, 7, "Name");
                                    string desc = GetWiaPropertyString(info.Properties, 4, "Description");

                                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name))
                                    {
                                        name = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(desc) ? desc : id;
                                    }

                                    list.Add(new ScannerDeviceInfo
                                    {
                                        DeviceId = id,
                                        Name = isAr ? $"{name} (متصل - WIA)" : $"{name} (Connected - WIA)",
                                        Description = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(desc)
                                            ? (isAr ? "ماسح ضوئي معرف عبر خدمة Windows WIA" : "Scanner detected via Windows WIA service")
                                            : desc
                                    });
                                }
                                catch
                                {
                                    // Skip malformed device entry
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"WIA Device Enumeration Warning: {ex.Message}");
                }

                // 2. Enumerate Pre-Defined / Installed Scanners in Windows Registry (StillImage Class {6bdd1fc6-810f-11d0-bec7-08002be2092f})
                try
                {
                    using var key = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey(@"SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Control\Class\{6bdd1fc6-810f-11d0-bec7-08002be2092f}");
                    if (key != null)
                    {
                        foreach (string subKeyName in key.GetSubKeyNames())
                        {
                            if (subKeyName.Length == 4 && int.TryParse(subKeyName, out _))
                            {
                                using var subKey = key.OpenSubKey(subKeyName);
                                string? driverDesc = subKey?.GetValue("DriverDesc")?.ToString();
                                string? friendlyName = subKey?.GetValue("FriendlyName")?.ToString() ?? driverDesc;
                                string? provider = subKey?.GetValue("ProviderName")?.ToString() ?? "Windows Imaging";

                                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(friendlyName) &&
                                    !list.Any(s => s.Name.Contains(friendlyName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                                {
                                    list.Add(new ScannerDeviceInfo
                                    {
                                        DeviceId = $"WIN_DEF_SCANNER_{subKeyName}",
                                        Name = isAr ? $"{friendlyName} (معرّف على الجهاز)" : $"{friendlyName} (Installed on PC)",
                                        Description = isAr ? $"المزود: {provider} - معرف في النظام" : $"Provider: {provider} - System Registered"
                                    });
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch
                {
                    // Ignore registry permission restrictions if any
                }

                // 3. Add Interactive Windows Scanner Selector option
                list.Add(new ScannerDeviceInfo
                {
                    DeviceId = InteractiveDialogDeviceId,
                    Name = isAr
                        ? "نافذة اختيار السكانر القياسية في الويندوز (Windows WIA / TWAIN Dialog)"
                        : "Standard Windows Scanner Selector (WIA / TWAIN Dialog)",
                    Description = isAr
                        ? "فتح مربع حوار أجهزة المسح الضوئي المعرفة في نظام التشغيل ويندوز مباشرة"
                        : "Opens native Windows scanning hardware dialog directly"
                });

                return list;
            });
        }

        public Task<byte[]> ScanDocumentAsync(ScanParameters parameters)
        {
            var tcs = new TaskCompletionSource<byte[]>(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);

            var thread = new Thread(() =>
            {
                bool isAr = LocalizationService.Instance.IsArabic;
                try
                {
                    byte[] bytes = ExecuteWiaScanOnStaThread(parameters, isAr);
                    tcs.TrySetResult(bytes);
                }
                catch (OperationCanceledException)
                {
                    tcs.TrySetCanceled();
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    var translated = TranslateWiaException(ex, isAr);
                    if (translated is OperationCanceledException)
                    {
                        tcs.TrySetCanceled();
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        tcs.TrySetException(translated);
                    }
                }
            });

            thread.IsBackground = true;
            thread.SetApartmentState(ApartmentState.STA);
            thread.Start();

            return tcs.Task;
        }

        private static byte[] ExecuteWiaScanOnStaThread(ScanParameters parameters, bool isAr)
        {
            var dialogType = Type.GetTypeFromProgID("WIA.CommonDialog");
            var mgrType = Type.GetTypeFromProgID("WIA.DeviceManager");
            if (dialogType == null || mgrType == null)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(isAr
                    ? "خدمة المسح الضوئي (Windows WIA) غير مفعلة على هذا النظام."
                    : "Windows Image Acquisition (WIA) service is not available on this system.");
            }

            dynamic? dialogObj = Activator.CreateInstance(dialogType);
            dynamic? deviceManagerObj = Activator.CreateInstance(mgrType);
            if (dialogObj is null || deviceManagerObj is null)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(isAr
                    ? "تعذر تهيئة مكونات المسح الضوئي في الويندوز."
                    : "Failed to initialize Windows scanning components.");
            }

            dynamic dialog = dialogObj;
            dynamic deviceManager = deviceManagerObj;

            dynamic? targetDevice = null;
            bool useInteractiveDialog = parameters.DeviceId == InteractiveDialogDeviceId;

            if (!useInteractiveDialog)
            {
                dynamic? firstDeviceInfo = null;
                foreach (dynamic info in deviceManager.DeviceInfos)
                {
                    firstDeviceInfo ??= info;
                    string devId = string.Empty;
                    try { devId = (string)info.DeviceID; } catch { }

                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(parameters.DeviceId) &&
                        string.Equals(devId, parameters.DeviceId, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        targetDevice = info.Connect();
                        break;
                    }
                }

                // Fallback to the first connected WIA scanner if WIN_DEF_SCANNER_* or legacy ID was stored
                if (targetDevice is null && firstDeviceInfo is not null)
                {
                    targetDevice = firstDeviceInfo.Connect();
                }
            }

            if (targetDevice is not null)
            {
                dynamic connectedDevice = targetDevice;
                ConfigureDeviceHandling(connectedDevice, parameters.UseFeeder);

                dynamic? itemObj = null;
                foreach (dynamic subItem in connectedDevice.Items)
                {
                    itemObj = subItem;
                    break;
                }

                if (itemObj is null)
                {
                    throw new InvalidOperationException(isAr
                        ? "لم يتم العثور على وحدة مسح ضوئي صالحة داخل جهاز السكانر المتصل."
                        : "No scannable item was found on the connected scanner device.");
                }

                dynamic item = itemObj;
                SetWiaProperty(item.Properties, 6146, (int)parameters.ColorMode); // Current Intent
                SetWiaProperty(item.Properties, 6147, parameters.ResolutionDpi);  // Horizontal Resolution
                SetWiaProperty(item.Properties, 6148, parameters.ResolutionDpi);  // Vertical Resolution

                dynamic? scannedImage = null;
                try
                {
                    scannedImage = dialog.ShowTransfer(item, WiaFormatBMP, false);
                }
                catch (Exception transferEx)
                {
                    if (IsCancelOrPaperEmptyError(transferEx))
                    {
                        throw;
                    }
                    // Fallback to direct transfer when UI transfer dialog fails on specific drivers
                    scannedImage = item.Transfer(WiaFormatBMP);
                }

                if (scannedImage is null)
                {
                    throw new OperationCanceledException(isAr
                        ? "تم إلغاء عملية المسح الضوئي."
                        : "Scanning operation was cancelled.");
                }

                return ExtractPngBytesFromWiaImage(scannedImage);
            }
            else
            {
                // Interactive Windows scanner selection dialog
                dynamic? imageFile = dialog.ShowAcquireImage(
                    1, // ScannerDeviceType
                    (int)parameters.ColorMode,
                    parameters.ResolutionDpi,
                    WiaFormatBMP,
                    false,
                    true,
                    false);

                if (imageFile is null)
                {
                    throw new OperationCanceledException(isAr
                        ? "تم إلغاء عملية المسح الضوئي."
                        : "Scanning operation was cancelled.");
                }

                return ExtractPngBytesFromWiaImage(imageFile);
            }
        }

        private static void ConfigureDeviceHandling(dynamic targetDevice, bool useFeeder)
        {
            try
            {
                int caps = GetWiaPropertyInt(targetDevice.Properties, 3086); // Document Handling Capabilities
                bool supportsFeeder = (caps & 1) != 0;
                bool supportsFlatbed = (caps & 2) != 0;

                if (useFeeder || (supportsFeeder && !supportsFlatbed))
                {
                    SetWiaProperty(targetDevice.Properties, 3088, 1); // Document Handling Select = Feeder
                    SetWiaProperty(targetDevice.Properties, 3096, 1); // Pages = 1
                }
                else if (!useFeeder && supportsFlatbed)
                {
                    SetWiaProperty(targetDevice.Properties, 3088, 2); // Document Handling Select = Flatbed
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                // Some scanner drivers manage document handling internally
            }
        }

        private static byte[] ExtractPngBytesFromWiaImage(dynamic wiaImageFile)
        {
            string tempPath = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"wia_scan_{Guid.NewGuid():N}.bmp");
            try
            {
                wiaImageFile.SaveFile(tempPath);

                using var bmp = new System.Drawing.Bitmap(tempPath);
                using var ms = new MemoryStream();
                bmp.Save(ms, System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat.Png);
                return ms.ToArray();
            }
            finally
            {
                try
                {
                    if (File.Exists(tempPath))
                    {
                        File.Delete(tempPath);
                    }
                }
                catch
                {
                    // Ignore temp cleanup failure
                }
            }
        }

        private static string GetWiaPropertyString(dynamic properties, int propId, string propName)
        {
            try
            {
                foreach (dynamic prop in properties)
                {
                    int id = 0;
                    string name = string.Empty;
                    try { id = Convert.ToInt32(prop.PropertyID); } catch { }
                    try { name = Convert.ToString(prop.Name) ?? string.Empty; } catch { }

                    if (id == propId || string.Equals(name, propName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        return Convert.ToString(prop.Value) ?? string.Empty;
                    }
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                // Ignore unreadable property collections
            }
            return string.Empty;
        }

        private static int GetWiaPropertyInt(dynamic properties, int propId)
        {
            try
            {
                foreach (dynamic prop in properties)
                {
                    int id = 0;
                    try { id = Convert.ToInt32(prop.PropertyID); } catch { }

                    if (id == propId)
                    {
                        return Convert.ToInt32(prop.Value);
                    }
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                // Ignore unreadable property
            }
            return 0;
        }

        private static void SetWiaProperty(dynamic properties, int propId, object val)
        {
            try
            {
                foreach (dynamic prop in properties)
                {
                    int id = 0;
                    try { id = Convert.ToInt32(prop.PropertyID); } catch { }

                    if (id == propId)
                    {
                        prop.Value = val;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                // Some scanner drivers restrict specific property writes
            }
        }

        private static COMException? FindComException(Exception ex)
        {
            Exception? current = ex;
            while (current != null)
            {
                if (current is COMException comEx)
                {
                    return comEx;
                }
                current = current.InnerException;
            }
            return null;
        }

        private static bool IsCancelOrPaperEmptyError(Exception ex)
        {
            var comEx = FindComException(ex);
            if (comEx == null) return false;
            return comEx.HResult == WIA_ERROR_USER_CANCELLED || comEx.HResult == WIA_ERROR_PAPER_EMPTY;
        }

        private static Exception TranslateWiaException(Exception ex, bool isAr)
        {
            var comEx = FindComException(ex);
            if (comEx != null)
            {
                switch (comEx.HResult)
                {
                    case WIA_ERROR_USER_CANCELLED:
                        return new OperationCanceledException(isAr
                            ? "تم إلغاء عملية المسح الضوئي."
                            : "Scanning operation was cancelled.");

                    case WIA_ERROR_PAPER_EMPTY:
                        return new Exception(isAr
                            ? "لا يوجد ورق في الماسح الضوئي (ساحب الأوراق فارغ). يرجى وضع الورقة في السكانر والمحاولة مرة أخرى."
                            : "No paper detected in the scanner feeder. Please load paper and try again.");

                    case WIA_ERROR_OFFLINE:
                        return new Exception(isAr
                            ? "الماسح الضوئي غير متصل أو قيد الإيقاف. يرجى التأكد من توصيل السكانر وتشغيله."
                            : "The scanner is offline or disconnected. Please turn it on and check the connection.");

                    case WIA_ERROR_BUSY:
                        return new Exception(isAr
                            ? "الماسح الضوئي مشغول حالياً. يرجى الانتظار قليلاً والمحاولة مرة أخرى."
                            : "The scanner is currently busy. Please wait a moment and try again.");

                    case WIA_S_NO_DEVICE_AVAILABLE:
                        return new Exception(isAr
                            ? "لم يتم العثور على أي جهاز ماسح ضوئي متصل بالكمبيوتر."
                            : "No connected scanner device was found.");
                }
            }

            return ex;
        }
    }
}
Views\ScannerDialog.xaml.cs
WIA hardware scanner selection dialog code-behind managing real device discovery, default scanner persistence, DPI/color/ADF parameters, and live scanned preview.
منطق نافذة اختيار السكانر الحقيقي وإعدادات الدقة ونمط الألوان والساحب الآلي (ADF) وعرض المعاينة الفورية للوثيقة الممسوحة.
using System;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using DocuArchivePro.Services;

namespace DocuArchivePro.Views
{
    public partial class ScannerDialog : Window, INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        private readonly IScannerService _scannerService;
        public ILocalizationService Loc => LocalizationService.Instance;

        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;

        public ObservableCollection<ScannerDeviceInfo> AvailableScanners { get; } = new();
        private ScannerDeviceInfo? _selectedScanner;
        public ScannerDeviceInfo? SelectedScanner
        {
            get => _selectedScanner;
            set
            {
                if (_selectedScanner != value)
                {
                    _selectedScanner = value;
                    OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SelectedScanner));
                }
            }
        }
        public byte[]? ScannedResultBytes { get; private set; }

        public ScannerDialog(IScannerService scannerService)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            DataContext = this;
            _scannerService = scannerService;
            Loaded += async (s, e) => await LoadScannersAndDefaultsAsync();
        }

        private async Task LoadScannersAndDefaultsAsync()
        {
            var scanners = await _scannerService.GetAvailableScannersAsync();
            AvailableScanners.Clear();
            foreach (var s in scanners) AvailableScanners.Add(s);

            var settings = await SecurityService.Instance.GetSettingsAsync();
            UpdateDefaultScannerBanner(settings.DefaultScannerName, settings.DefaultScanDpi);

            // Restore saved DPI
            if (settings.DefaultScanDpi == 150) CmbDpi.SelectedIndex = 0;
            else if (settings.DefaultScanDpi == 600) CmbDpi.SelectedIndex = 2;
            else CmbDpi.SelectedIndex = 1;

            // Restore saved ColorMode
            if (settings.DefaultScanColorMode == 2) CmbColorMode.SelectedIndex = 1;
            else if (settings.DefaultScanColorMode == 4) CmbColorMode.SelectedIndex = 2;
            else CmbColorMode.SelectedIndex = 0;

            ChkUseFeeder.IsChecked = settings.UseScannerFeeder;

            var matched = (!string.Equals(settings.DefaultScannerDeviceId, "VIRTUAL_TEST_SCANNER_01", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                           !string.Equals(settings.DefaultScannerDeviceId, WiaScannerService.InteractiveDialogDeviceId, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                ? AvailableScanners.FirstOrDefault(s => s.DeviceId == settings.DefaultScannerDeviceId)
                : null;

            matched ??= AvailableScanners.FirstOrDefault(s => s.DeviceId != WiaScannerService.InteractiveDialogDeviceId)
                        ?? AvailableScanners.FirstOrDefault();

            if (matched != null)
            {
                SelectedScanner = matched;
                UpdateDefaultScannerBanner(matched.Name, settings.DefaultScanDpi);
            }
        }

        private void UpdateDefaultScannerBanner(string scannerName, int dpi)
        {
            bool isAr = Loc.IsArabic;
            string text = isAr
                ? $"السكانر المعتمد حالياً: {scannerName} ({dpi} DPI)"
                : $"Active Default Scanner: {scannerName} ({dpi} DPI)";
            TxtDefaultScannerInfo.Text = DateTimeHelper.FormatDigits(text, isAr);
        }

        private void OnPropertyChanged(string name)
        {
            PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(name));
        }

        private async void BtnRefreshScanners_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            await LoadScannersAndDefaultsAsync();
            bool isAr = Loc.IsArabic;
            string msg = isAr
                ? $"تم فحص وتحديث قائمة أجهزة السكانر المعرفة على الجهاز (عدد الخيارات المتاحة: {DateTimeHelper.FormatNumber(AvailableScanners.Count, true)})."
                : $"Scanner devices list refreshed (available devices: {AvailableScanners.Count}).";
            MessageBox.Show(msg, Loc["Success"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
        }

        private async void BtnSaveDefaultScanner_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            bool isAr = Loc.IsArabic;
            if (SelectedScanner == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    isAr ? "يرجى اختيار جهاز سكانر من القائمة أولاً." : "Please select a scanner device from the list first.",
                    Loc["Warning"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
                return;
            }

            int dpi = 300;
            if (CmbDpi.SelectedIndex == 0) dpi = 150;
            else if (CmbDpi.SelectedIndex == 2) dpi = 600;

            int colorMode = 1;
            if (CmbColorMode.SelectedIndex == 1) colorMode = 2;
            else if (CmbColorMode.SelectedIndex == 2) colorMode = 4;

            var settings = await SecurityService.Instance.GetSettingsAsync();
            settings.DefaultScannerDeviceId = SelectedScanner.DeviceId;
            settings.DefaultScannerName = SelectedScanner.Name;
            settings.DefaultScanDpi = dpi;
            settings.DefaultScanColorMode = colorMode;
            settings.UseScannerFeeder = ChkUseFeeder.IsChecked == true;

            await SecurityService.Instance.SaveSettingsAsync(settings);
            UpdateDefaultScannerBanner(settings.DefaultScannerName, settings.DefaultScanDpi);

            MessageBox.Show(
                isAr
                    ? $"تم حفظ السكانر ({SelectedScanner.Name}) كماسح ضوئي افتراضي للبرنامج بنجاح!"
                    : $"Scanner ({SelectedScanner.Name}) saved as the default device!",
                Loc["Success"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
        }

        private async void BtnScan_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                int dpi = 300;
                if (CmbDpi.SelectedIndex == 0) dpi = 150;
                else if (CmbDpi.SelectedIndex == 2) dpi = 600;

                ScanColorMode mode = ScanColorMode.Color;
                if (CmbColorMode.SelectedIndex == 1) mode = ScanColorMode.Grayscale;
                else if (CmbColorMode.SelectedIndex == 2) mode = ScanColorMode.BlackAndWhite;

                var parameters = new ScanParameters
                {
                    DeviceId = SelectedScanner?.DeviceId,
                    ResolutionDpi = dpi,
                    ColorMode = mode,
                    UseFeeder = ChkUseFeeder.IsChecked == true,
                    OutputFormat = "PNG"
                };

                IsEnabled = false;
                ScannedResultBytes = await _scannerService.ScanDocumentAsync(parameters);

                using var ms = new MemoryStream(ScannedResultBytes);
                var bitmap = new BitmapImage();
                bitmap.BeginInit();
                bitmap.CacheOption = BitmapCacheOption.OnLoad;
                bitmap.StreamSource = ms;
                bitmap.EndInit();
                bitmap.Freeze();

                ImgPreview.Source = bitmap;
                TxtPlaceholder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
                BtnAccept.IsEnabled = true;
            }
            catch (OperationCanceledException)
            {
                // User cancelled interactive dialog
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, Loc["Error"], MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
            }
            finally
            {
                IsEnabled = true;
            }
        }

        private void BtnAccept_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            DialogResult = true;
            Close();
        }

        private void BtnCancel_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            DialogResult = false;
            Close();
        }
    }
}
Views\MergeEntitiesDialog.xaml

<Window x:Class="DocuArchivePro.Views.MergeEntitiesDialog"

                    <!-- Information Alert -->
